	makefolder("TayHub")

_G.SaveData = _G.SaveData or {}
SaveSettings = function()
	if writefile then
		return (pcall(function()
			local json = HttpService:JSONEncode(_G.SaveData)

			writefile("TayHub/cu.json", json)
		end))
	end

	return false
end
LoadSettings = function()
	if isfile then
		if isfile("TayHub/cu.json") then
			local ok, saveData = pcall(function()
				local json = readfile("TayHub/cu.json")

				return HttpService:JSONDecode(json)
			end)

			if ok and saveData then
				_G.SaveData = saveData

				return true
			end

			return false
		end
	end

	return false
end
GetSetting = function(p1, p2)
	return _G.SaveData[p1] ~= nil and _G.SaveData[p1] or p2
end
LoadSettings()
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(60) do
		SaveSettings()
	end
end)

EquipWeapon = function(p3)
	if not p3 then
		return
	end

	if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p3) then
		plr.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p3))
	end
end
weaponSc = function(p4)
	for _, child in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") and child.ToolTip == p4 then
			EquipWeapon(child.Name)
		end
	end
end

local t9 = {}

t9.__index = t9
t9.Alive = function(p5)
	if not p5 then
		return
	end

	local Humanoid = p5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	return Humanoid and Humanoid.Health > 0
end
t9.Pos = function(p6, p7)
	return (Root.Position - mode.Position).Magnitude <= p7
end
t9.Dist = function(p8, p9)
	return (Root.Position - p8:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position).Magnitude <= p9
end
t9.DistH = function(p10, p11)
	return (Root.Position - p10:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position).Magnitude > p11
end
t9.Kill = function(p12, p13)
	if p12 and p13 then
		if not p12:GetAttribute("Locked") then
			p12:SetAttribute("Locked", p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		end

		PosMon = p12:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
		BringEnemy()
		EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)

		if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").ToolTip == "Blox Fruit" then
			_tp(p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0))
		else
			_tp(p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
		end

		if RandomCFrame then
			wait(0.5)
			_tp(p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
			wait(0.5)
			_tp(p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
			wait(0.5)
			_tp(p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
			wait(0.5)
			_tp(p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
			wait(0.5)
			_tp(p12.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
		end
	end
end

t9.Mas = function(p20, p21)
	if p20 and p21 then
		if not p20:GetAttribute("Locked") then
			p20:SetAttribute("Locked", p20.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
		end

		PosMon = p20:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
		BringEnemy()

		if p20.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
			_tp(p20.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
			Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
			Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
			Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
		else
			weaponSc("Melee")
			_tp(p20.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
		end
	end
end

statsSetings = function(p24, p25)
	if p24 ~= "Melee" then
		if p24 ~= "Defense" then
			if p24 ~= "Sword" then
				if p24 ~= "Gun" then
					if p24 == "Devil" and plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Demon Fruit", p25)
					end
				elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
					replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Gun", p25)
				end
			elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
				replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Sword", p25)
			end
		elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
			replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Defense", p25)
		end
	elseif plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
		replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Melee", p25)
	end
end
BringEnemy = function(p26)
	if not _B then
		return
	end

	if not p26 then
		local HumanoidRootPart = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not HumanoidRootPart then
			return
		end

		local huge = math.huge

		for _, child in ipairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
			local Humanoid = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local HumanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if Humanoid and HumanoidRootPart2 and Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart2.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

				if Magnitude < huge then
					huge = Magnitude
					p26 = child
				end
			end
		end

		if not p26 then
			return
		end
	end

	local u26 = false

	local function v27(p27)
		local Humanoid = p27:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local HumanoidRootPart = p27:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local v30 = Humanoid and HumanoidRootPart

		return v30 and Humanoid.Health > 0, HumanoidRootPart, Humanoid
	end
	local function v31(p28)
		if isnetworkowner then
			return isnetworkowner(p28)
		end

		return p28.ReceiveAge == 0 and not p28.Anchored and p28.Velocity.Magnitude > 0
	end

	pcall(function()
		if sethiddenproperty then
			sethiddenproperty(plr, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
		end

		local Position = p26.HumanoidRootPart.Position

		for _, child in ipairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
			if child ~= p26 then
				local v35, v36, v37 = v27(child)

				if v35 and child.Name == p26.Name then
					local Magnitude = (v36.Position - Position).Magnitude

					if Magnitude <= 3000 then
						if not v36:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity") then
							local BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")

							BodyVelocity.Name = "BodyVelocity"
							BodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
							BodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
							BodyVelocity.Parent = v36
						end

						if Magnitude <= 10 then
							u26 = true
						end

						if not u26 and v31(v36) then
							v36.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position)
						end

						v36.CanCollide = false
						v37.WalkSpeed = 0
						v37.JumpPower = 0
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if p26 and p26:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			p26.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
			p26.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
			p26.Humanoid.JumpPower = 0
		end
	end)
end
Useskills = function(p29, p30)
	if p29 == "Melee" then
		weaponSc("Melee")

		if p30 == "Z" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
		elseif p30 == "X" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
		elseif p30 == "C" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game)
		end
	elseif p29 == "Sword" then
		weaponSc("Sword")

		if p30 == "Z" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
		elseif p30 == "X" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
		end
	elseif p29 == "Blox Fruit" then
		weaponSc("Blox Fruit")

		if p30 == "Z" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
		elseif p30 == "X" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
		elseif p30 == "C" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game)
		elseif p30 == "V" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "V", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "V", false, game)
		end
	elseif p29 == "Gun" then
		weaponSc("Gun")

		if p30 == "Z" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
		elseif p30 == "X" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
		end
	end

	if p29 == "nil" then
		if p30 == "Y" then
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Y", false, game)
			vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Y", false, game)
		end
	end
end

local v40 = getrawmetatable(game)
local __namecall = v40.__namecall

setreadonly(v40, false)
v40.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
	local v42 = getnamecallmethod()
	local t10 = { ... }

	if tostring(v42) ~= "FireServer" or tostring(t10[1]) ~= "RemoteEvent" or tostring(t10[2]) == "true" or tostring(t10[2]) == "false" or (not _G.FarmMastery_G or SoulGuitar) and not _G.FarmMastery_Dev and not _G.FarmBlazeEM and not _G.Prehis_Skills and not _G.SeaBeast1 and not _G.FishBoat and not _G.PGB and not _G.Leviathan1 and not _G.Complete_Trials and (not _G.AimMethod or ABmethod ~= "Aim Player") and (not _G.AimMethod or ABmethod ~= "Nearest Aim") then
		return __namecall(...)
	end

	t10[2] = MousePos

	return __namecall(unpack(t10))
end)
GetConnectionEnemies = function(p31)
	for _, child in pairs(replicated:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Model") then
			if (typeof(p31) ~= "table" or not table.find(p31, child.Name)) and child.Name ~= p31 then
				continue
			end

			if not child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
				continue
			end

			if child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				return child
			end
		end
	end

	local children, v47 = game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, v47 do
		if v:IsA("Model") and (typeof(p31) == "table" and table.find(p31, v.Name) or v.Name == p31) and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
			return v
		end
	end
end
LowCpu = function()
	local Terrain = game.Workspace.Terrain

	Terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
	Terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
	Terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
	Terrain.WaterTransparency = 0
	game.Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
	game.Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9
	game.Lighting.Brightness = 0
	settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"

	for _, descendant in pairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("Part") or descendant:IsA("Union") or descendant:IsA("CornerWedgePart") or descendant:IsA("TrussPart") then
			descendant.Material = "Plastic"
			descendant.Reflectance = 0
		else
			if not descendant:IsA("Decal") then
				if not descendant:IsA("Texture") then
					if descendant:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or descendant:IsA("Trail") then
						descendant.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
					elseif descendant:IsA("Explosion") then
						descendant.BlastPressure = 1
						descendant.BlastRadius = 1
					else
						if not descendant:IsA("Fire") then
							if not descendant:IsA("SpotLight") and not descendant:IsA("Smoke") and not descendant:IsA("Sparkles") then
								if not descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
									continue
								end

								descendant.Material = "Plastic"
								descendant.Reflectance = 0
								descendant.TextureID = 10385902758728956

								continue
							end
						end

						descendant.Enabled = false
					end

					continue
				end
			end

			descendant.Transparency = 1
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(game.Lighting:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("BlurEffect") or child:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or child:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or child:IsA("BloomEffect") or child:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
			child.Enabled = false
		end
	end
end

DropFruits = function()
	local children, v79 = plr.Backpack:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, v79 do
		if string.find(v.Name, "Fruit") then
			EquipWeapon(v.Name)
			wait(0.1)

			if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
				plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
			end

			EquipWeapon(v.Name)
			plr.Character:FindFirstChild(v.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer("Drop")
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if string.find(child.Name, "Fruit") then
			EquipWeapon(child.Name)
			wait(0.1)

			if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
				plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
			end

			EquipWeapon(child.Name)
			plr.Character:FindFirstChild(child.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer("Drop")
		end
	end
end

getInfinity_Ability = function(p37, p38)
	if not Root then
		return
	end

	if p37 == "Soru" and p38 then
		local next_ = next
		local v91, v92 = getgc()

		for _, v in next_, v91, v92 do
			if plr.Character.Soru and typeof(v) == "function" and getfenv(v).script == plr.Character.Soru then
				local next_2 = next
				local v96, v97 = getupvalues(v)

				for _, v2 in next_2, v96, v97 do
					if typeof(v2) == "table" then
						repeat
							wait(Sec)
							v2.LastUse = 0
						until plr.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0
					end
				end
			end
		end
	elseif p37 == "Energy" and p38 then
		plr.Character.Energy.Changed:connect(function()
			if p38 then
				plr.Character.Energy.Value = Energy
			end
		end)
	elseif p37 == "Observation" and p38 then
		plr.VisionRadius.Value = math.huge
	end
end
Hop = function()
	pcall(function()
		for i = math.random(1, math.random(40, 75)), 100 do
			for k, v in next, replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(i), nil do
				if tonumber(v.Count) < 12 then
					TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, k)
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

local Part = Instance.new("Part", workspace)

Part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
Part.Name = "Rip_Indra"
Part.Anchored = true
Part.CanCollide = false
Part.CanTouch = false
Part.Transparency = 1

local PartName = workspace:FindFirstChild(Part.Name)

if PartName and PartName ~= Part then
	PartName:Destroy()
end

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait() do
		if Part and Part.Parent == workspace then
			if shouldTween then
				getgenv().OnFarm = true
			else
				getgenv().OnFarm = false
			end
		else
			getgenv().OnFarm = false
		end
	end
end)
task.spawn(function()
	local LocalPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer

	while true do
		task.wait()

		if not LocalPlayer.Character then
			continue
		end

		if LocalPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart then
			break
		end
	end

	Part.CFrame = LocalPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame

	while task.wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if getgenv().OnFarm then
				if Part and Part.Parent == workspace then
					local PrimaryPart = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character.PrimaryPart

					if PrimaryPart and (PrimaryPart.Position - Part.Position).Magnitude <= 200 then
						PrimaryPart.CFrame = Part.CFrame
					else
						Part.CFrame = PrimaryPart.CFrame
					end
				end

				local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

				if Character then
					for _, child in pairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") then
							child.CanCollide = false
						end
					end
				end
			else
				local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

				if Character then
					for _, child in pairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("BasePart") and (child.Name == "Torso" or child.Name == "UpperTorso" or child.Name == "LowerTorso" or child.Name == "Head") then
							child.CanCollide = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

game:GetService("TweenService")
game:GetService("RunService")

local LocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

sea1 = game.PlaceId == 2753915549 or game.PlaceId == 85211729168715
sea2 = game.PlaceId == 4442272183 or game.PlaceId == 79091703265657
sea3 = game.PlaceId == 7449423635 or game.PlaceId == 100117331123089

local t11 = {
	["Tween Speed"] = 100,
	["Bypass Teleport"] = false,
	["Up Y"] = false,
	["Up Y When Low Health"] = false,
	["Same Y"] = false
}
local cFrame = CFrame.new(10641.0918, -1953.92981, 9825.07031, -0.652825892, -9.2805891e-08, -0.757508039, -2.73638356e-08, 1, -9.89323823e-08, 0.757508039, -4.38572947e-08, -0.652825892)
local cFrame2 = CFrame.new(-16271.126, 25.5847301, 1371.98755, 0.999396622, -5.78875188e-08, -0.0347310975, 5.52972779e-08, 1, -8.7544322e-08, 0.034731105, 8.28877091e-08, 0.999396741)

Convert_CFrame = function(p39)
	if not p39 then
		return
	end

	if typeof(p39) == "Vector3" then
		return CFrame.new(p39)
	end

	if typeof(p39) == "CFrame" then
		return p39
	end

	if typeof(p39) == "Model" then
		return p39:GetPivot()
	end

	if p39.CFrame then
		return p39.CFrame
	end

	return nil
end
GetDistance = function(p40, p41, p42)
	if p40 == nil then
		return 9e9
	end

	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

	if not Character then
		return 9e9
	end

	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	if not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		return 9e9
	end

	if p41 == nil then
		p41 = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if not p41 then
			return 9e9
		end
	end

	local v120 = Convert_CFrame(p40)
	local v121 = Convert_CFrame(p41)

	if p42 then
		return (Vector3.new(v120.X, 0, v120.Z) - Vector3.new(v121.X, 0, v121.Z)).Magnitude
	end

	return (v120.Position - v121.Position).Magnitude
end
InArea = function(p43)
	local _WorldOrigin = workspace:FindFirstChild("_WorldOrigin")

	if not _WorldOrigin then
		return {
			Name = ""
		}
	end

	local v123 = Convert_CFrame(p43)
	local children, v125 = _WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, v125 do
		if v:FindFirstChild("Mesh") then
			if (v123.Position - v.Position).Magnitude <= v.Mesh.Scale.X then
				return v
			end
		end
	end

	return {
		Name = ""
	}
end
GetSpawnPoint = function(p44)
	local Pirates_ = workspace:FindFirstChild("_WorldOrigin") and workspace._WorldOrigin:FindFirstChild("PlayerSpawns") and workspace._WorldOrigin.PlayerSpawns:FindFirstChild("Pirates")

	if not Pirates_ then
		return
	end

	local children, v130 = Pirates_:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, v130 do
		if v:FindFirstChild("Part") then
			if (v.Part.Position - p44.Position).Magnitude <= 2500 then
				return v
			end
		end
	end
end
CheckLegendaryItems = function()
	local function v133(p45)
		local children, v135 = LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()

		for _, v in next, children, v135 do
			if v:IsA("Tool") then
				if v.Name == p45 or string.find(v.Name, p45) then
					return v
				end
			end
		end

		local children2, v139 = LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()

		for _, v in next, children2, v139 do
			if v:IsA("Tool") then
				if v.Name == p45 or string.find(v.Name, p45) then
					return v
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if v133("God's Chalice") or v133("Fist of Darkness") or v133("Sweet Chalice") or v133("Hallow Essence") or v133("Flower1") then
		return true
	end

	return false
end
WaitForHumanoid = function()
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

	if not Character then
		return nil
	end

	local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	if Humanoid then
		return Humanoid
	end

	local v144 = tick() + 5

	while tick() < v144 do
		local Humanoid2 = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

		if Humanoid2 then
			return Humanoid2
		end

		task.wait(0.1)
	end

	return nil
end
checkinventory = function(p46)
	if p46 then
		for _, v in pairs(ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")) do
			if v.Name == p46 then
				return true
			end
		end
	end

	return false
end
getdis = function(p47, p48)
	local CFrame_ = p48 or LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame

	return (CFrame.new(p47.X, CFrame_.Y, p47.Z).Position - CFrame.new(CFrame_.X, CFrame_.Y, CFrame_.Z).Position).Magnitude
end
CanBypassTeleport = function(p49)
	local Name = InArea(p49).Name

	if Name == "" then
		return false
	end

	if t11["Bypass Teleport"] and not Name:find("Dimension") and not Name:find("Submerged") and Name ~= "Sealed Cavern" then
		if not Name:lower():find("under") and not CheckLegendaryItems() then
			if LocalPlayer.Data and LocalPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint and LocalPlayer.Data.LastSpawnPoint.Value == "SubmergedIsland" then
				return false
			end

			if GetDistance(p49.Position) <= 3500 then
				return false
			end

			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end
GetBypassCFrame = function(p50)
	local huge = math.huge
	local v151
	local children = workspace._WorldOrigin.PlayerSpawns.Pirates:GetChildren()
	local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not HumanoidRootPart then
		return nil
	end

	for _, v in next, children, nil do
		if v:FindFirstChild("Part") and (p50.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude >= 3000 and GetSpawnPoint(v.Part) ~= GetSpawnPoint(HumanoidRootPart) and (v.Part.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10000 and (v.Part.Position - p50.Position).Magnitude <= huge then
			huge = (v.Part.Position - p50.Position).Magnitude
			v151 = v
		end
	end

	return v151
end
BypassTP = function(p51)
	local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

	if not Character then
		return
	end

	local v157 = WaitForHumanoid()

	if not v157 or v157.Health <= 0 then
		return
	end

	if CanBypassTeleport(p51) and GetBypassCFrame(p51) then
		local v158 = GetBypassCFrame(p51)

		if v158 and v158:FindFirstChild("Part") then
			Character.LastSpawnPoint.Disabled = true
			ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("SetLastSpawnPoint", v158.Name)
			ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("SetSpawnPoint")
			Character:PivotTo(v158.Part.CFrame)
			v157:ChangeState(15)

			while true do
				task.wait()

				if not LocalPlayer.Character then
					continue
				end

				if WaitForHumanoid() and WaitForHumanoid().Health > 0 then
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
totopofgreattree = function()
	if getdis(CFrame.new(28310.0234, 14895.1123, 109.456741)) > 1500 then
		ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("requestEntrance", Vector3.new(28310.0234, 14895.1123, 109.456741))
		wait(0.3)
	end

	local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(28607.5352, 14896.5449, 106.011726)

	_tp(cFrame3)

	repeat
		wait()
	until getdis(cFrame3) <= 5

	wait(0.5)

	for i = 1, 4 do
		ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "TeleportBack")
	end
end
requestentrance = function(p52)
	local Position = p52

	if typeof(p52) == "CFrame" then
		Position = p52.Position
	end

	local t12

	if sea1 then
		t12 = {
			Sky3 = Vector3.new(-7894, 5547, -380),
			Sky3Exit = Vector3.new(-4607, 874, -1667),
			UnderWater = Vector3.new(61163, 11, 1819),
			["Underwater City"] = Vector3.new(61165.19140625, 0.18704631924629211, 1897.379150390625),
			["Pirate Village"] = Vector3.new(-1242.4625244140625, 4.7870597839355469, 3901.282958984375),
			UnderwaterExit = Vector3.new(4050, -1, -1814)
		}
	elseif sea2 then
		t12 = {
			["Swan Mansion"] = Vector3.new(-390, 332, 673),
			["Swan Room"] = Vector3.new(2285, 15, 905),
			["Cursed Ship"] = Vector3.new(923, 126, 32852),
			["Zombie Island"] = Vector3.new(-6509, 83, -133)
		}
	else
		t12 = {
			["Hydra Island"] = Vector3.new(5657.88623046875, 1013.0790405273438, -335.4996337890625),
			Mansion = Vector3.new(-12462, 375, -7552),
			Castle = Vector3.new(-5036, 315, -3179),
			["Temple of Time"] = Vector3.new(28286, 14897, 103),
			["Greate Tree"] = Vector3.new(3024.1709, 2280.69434, -7325.12793)
		}

		if not checkinventory("Valkyrie Helm") then
			return
		end
	end

	local v163
	local huge = math.huge

	for _, v in pairs(t12) do
		local Magnitude = typeof(v) == "Vector3" and (v - Position).Magnitude or (v.Position - Position).Magnitude

		if Magnitude < huge then
			huge = Magnitude
			v163 = v
		end
	end

	if v163 and huge and huge < getdis(p52) then
		pcall(function()
			if _G.TweenCache then
				_G.TweenCache:Cancel()
			end
		end)

		local ok = typeof(v163) ~= "Vector3" or v163.X ~= 3024.1709 or v163.Y ~= 2280.69434 or v163.Z ~= -7325.12793

		if not ok then
			ok = not (ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaceV4Progress", "Check") >= 2)
		end

		if ok then
			if huge < getdis(p52) then
				ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("requestEntrance", typeof(v163) == "Vector3" and v163 or v163.Position)
				wait(1)
			end
		else
			totopofgreattree()
			wait(1)
		end
	end
end
_tp = function(p53)
	local cFrame3

	if typeof(p53) == "Vector3" then
		cFrame3 = CFrame.new(p53)
	elseif typeof(p53) == "CFrame" then
		cFrame3 = p53
	else
		cFrame3 = p53 and p53.CFrame
	end

	if not cFrame3 then
		return
	end

	if not plr.Character or not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		return
	end

	local HumanoidRootPart = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart

	pcall(function()
		if CanBypassTeleport(cFrame3) then
			BypassTP(cFrame3)
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		requestentrance(p53)
	end)

	if sea3 and getdis(cFrame3.Position, cFrame.Position) < 2000 then
		local HumanoidRootPart2 = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart

		if math.abs(cFrame.Position.Y - HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame.Y) > 1000 then
			repeat
				task.wait()
				old_tp(cFrame2)

				if getdis(cFrame2) < 10 then
					local Net = ReplicatedStorage.Modules.Net

					Net["RF/SubmarineWorkerSpeak"]:InvokeServer("AskKilledTikiBoss")
					task.wait(0.5)
					Net["RF/SubmarineWorkerSpeak"]:InvokeServer("TravelToSubmergedIsland")
				end
			until getdis(cFrame3.Position) < 2000

			task.wait(0.6)
			pcall(function()
				if HumanoidRootPart2:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
					HumanoidRootPart2.BodyClip:Destroy()
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new((cFrame3.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude / 300, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
	local tween_ = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(Part, tweenInfo, {
		CFrame = cFrame3
	})

	if plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == true then
		Part.CFrame = CFrame.new(Part.Position.X, cFrame3.Y, Part.Position.Z)
	end

	tween_:Play()
	task.spawn(function()
		while tween_.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing do
			if not shouldTween then
				tween_:Cancel()

				break
			end

			task.wait(0.1)
		end
	end)

	return tween_
end
old_tp = function(p54)
	if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p54
	end
end
TeleportToTarget = function(p55)
	if (p55.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 1000 then
		_tp(p55)
	else
		_tp(p55)
	end
end
notween = function(p56)
	plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p56
end
BTP = function(p57)
	local LocalPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer2.Character.HumanoidRootPart
	local Humanoid = LocalPlayer2.Character.Humanoid
	local Main = LocalPlayer2.PlayerGui.Main
	local Position = HumanoidRootPart.Position

	repeat
		Humanoid.Health = 0
		HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p57
		Main.Quest.Visible = false

		if (HumanoidRootPart.Position - Position).Magnitude > 1 then
			Position = HumanoidRootPart.Position
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p57
		end

		task.wait(0.5)
	until (p57.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 2000
end
spawn(function()

				end

					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						descendant.CanCollide = false
					end
				end
			else
				shouldTween = false

				if plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("BodyClip"):Destroy()
				end

				if plr.Character:FindFirstChild("highlight") then
					plr.Character:FindFirstChild("highlight"):Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

QuestBeta = function()
	QuestB()

	return {
		[0] = _G.FindBoss,

local Quests = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Quests"))
local GuideModule = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("GuideModule"))
local t13 = {
	"MarineQuest",
	"BartiloQuest",
	"CitizenQuest",
	"Trainees"
}

CheckSea = function(p58)
	if (game.PlaceId == 2753915549 or game.PlaceId == 85211729168715) and p58 == 1 then
		return true
	end

	if (game.PlaceId == 4442272183 or game.PlaceId == 79091703265657) and p58 == 2 then
		return true
	end

	if (game.PlaceId == 7449423635 or game.PlaceId == 100117331123089) and p58 == 3 then
		return true
	end

	return false
end
GetQuestPointFromNPC = function(p59)
	for _, child in pairs(workspace.NPCs:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == p59 and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			return child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(replicated.NPCs:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == p59 then
			if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				return child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end
GetQuests = function()
	local Value_ = plr.Data.Level.Value
	local n1 = 0
	local t14 = {}

	if Value_ >= 700 and CheckSea(1) then
		t14.Mob = "Galley Captain"
		t14.NameQuest = "FountainQuest"
		t14.ID = 2
		t14.LevelReq = 700
	elseif Value_ >= 1500 and CheckSea(2) then
		t14.Mob = "Water Fighter"
		t14.NameQuest = "ForgottenQuest"
		t14.ID = 2
		t14.LevelReq = 1450
	else
		for k, Quest in pairs(Quests) do
			for k2, v in pairs(Quest) do
				local LevelReq = v.LevelReq

				for k3 in pairs(v.Task) do
					if LevelReq <= Value_ and n1 <= LevelReq and v.Task[k3] > 1 and not table.find(t13, k) then
						n1 = LevelReq
						t14.Mob = k3
						t14.NameQuest = k
						t14.ID = k2
						t14.LevelReq = LevelReq
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	return t14
end
GetQuestPoint = function()
	if GuideModule and GuideModule.Data and GuideModule.Data.LastClosestNPC then
		return GetQuestPointFromNPC(GuideModule.Data.LastClosestNPC)
	end

	return nil
end
MaterialMon = function()
	local LocalPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
	local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not HumanoidRootPart then
		return
	end

	shouldRequestEntrance = function(p60, p61)
		if p61 <= (HumanoidRootPart.Position - p60).Magnitude then
			replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("requestEntrance", p60)
		end
	end

	if World1 then
		if SelectMaterial == "Angel Wings" then
			MMon = {
				"Shanda",
				"Royal Squad",
				"Royal Soldier",
				"Wysper",
				"Thunder God"
			}

			SP = "Default"
			shouldRequestEntrance(Vector3.new(-4607.82275, 872.54248, -1667.55688), 10000)
		elseif SelectMaterial == "Leather + Scrap Metal" then
			MMon = {
				"Brute",
				"Pirate"
			}
			MPos = CFrame.new(-1145, 15, 4350)
			SP = "Default"
		elseif SelectMaterial == "Magma Ore" then
			MMon = {
				"Military Soldier",
				"Military Spy",
				"Magma Admiral"
			}
			MPos = CFrame.new(-5815, 84, 8820)
			SP = "Default"
		elseif SelectMaterial == "Fish Tail" then
			MMon = {
				"Fishman Warrior",
				"Fishman Commando",
				"Fishman Lord"
			}
			MPos = CFrame.new(61123, 19, 1569)
			SP = "Default"
			shouldRequestEntrance(Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 5.3423423767089844, 1819.7841796875), 17000)
		end
	elseif World2 then
		if SelectMaterial == "Leather + Scrap Metal" then
			MMon = { "Marine Captain" }
			MPos = CFrame.new(-2010.5059814453125, 73.00115966796875, -3326.620849609375)
			SP = "Default"
		elseif SelectMaterial == "Magma Ore" then
			MMon = {
				"Magma Ninja",
				"Lava Pirate"
			}
			MPos = CFrame.new(-5428, 78, -5959)
			SP = "Default"

			}
			MPos = CFrame.new(620.63446044921875, 78.936447143554688, -12581.369140625)
			SP = "Default"
		elseif SelectMaterial == "Dragon Scale" then

	}
end

local u243 = false
local u244 = false

local function v245()
	if not plr.Character then
		return false
	end

	local HumanoidRootPart = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

	if not HumanoidRootPart then
		return false
	end

	local vector3 = Vector3.new(11520.8017578125, 0, 9829.513671875)

	return (Vector3.new(HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, 0, HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z) - vector3).Magnitude < 2000
end

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(Sec) do
		if _G.Level then
			pcall(function()
				local HumanoidRootPart = (plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()):WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not HumanoidRootPart then
					return
				end

				local Value_ = plr.Data.Level.Value
				local v250 = v245()
				local Quest = plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest
				local Text = Quest.Visible and Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text or ""

				if not (Value_ >= 2600) or v250 or u244 or u243 then
					if v250 or Value_ < 2600 then
						u243 = true
						u244 = false

						local v253 = QuestNeta()

						if not v253 or not v253[1] then
							task.wait(1)

							return
						end

						if Quest.Visible and not string.find(Text, v253[1]) then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
							task.wait(0.2)

							return
						end

						if not Quest.Visible then
							local v254 = v253[6]

							if v254 then
								_tp(v254)
								task.wait(2)

								if (HumanoidRootPart.Position - v254.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
									pcall(function()
										replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", v253[3], v253[2])
									end)
									task.wait(1)
								end
							else
								pcall(function()
									replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", v253[3], v253[2])
								end)
								task.wait(1)
							end

							return
						end

						local v255 = v253[1]
						local v256 = false
						local exitTo
						local v258

						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == v255 and t9.Alive(child) then
								v258 = child
								exitTo = 1

								break
							end
						end

						if exitTo == 1 then
							v256 = true

							while true do
								task.wait(Sec)
								_tp(v258.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
								t9.Kill(v258, _G.Level)

								if not Quest.Visible then
									break
								elseif not _G.Level or not v258.Parent or v258.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end

						if not v256 then
							for _, child in pairs(replicated:GetChildren()) do
								if child.Name == v255 and t9.Alive(child) then
									v256 = true
									_tp(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))

									break
								end
							end
						end

						if not v256 then
							for _, child in pairs(workspace._WorldOrigin.EnemySpawns:GetChildren()) do
								if string.find(child.Name, v255) then
									_tp(child.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))

									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				else
					u244 = true

					local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16269.7041, 25.2288494, 1373.65955)
					local n2 = 0

					while true do
						task.wait(Sec)
						_tp(cFrame3)
						n2 += 1

						if _G.Level then
							if (HumanoidRootPart.Position - cFrame3.Position).Magnitude <= 8 or n2 > 20 then
								break
							end
						else
							break
						end
					end

					if not _G.Level then
						u244 = false

						return
					end

					task.wait(1)
					pcall(function()
						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RF/SubmarineWorkerSpeak"):InvokeServer(unpack({ "TravelToSubmergedIsland" }))
					end)

					local now = tick()

					while true do
						task.wait(0.5)

						local v268 = v245()
						local v269 = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - cFrame3.Position).Magnitude > 50

						if v268 then
							break
						elseif v269 or not _G.Level or tick() - now > 15 then
							break
						end
					end

					task.wait(2)
					u243 = true
					u244 = false
				end
			end)
		else
			u244 = false
			u243 = false
		end
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.AutoFarmNear then
				for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
					if (child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						while true do
							wait()
							t9.Kill(child, _G.AutoFarmNear)

							if _G.AutoFarmNear then
								if not child.Parent then
									break
								end

								if child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end

								continue
							end

							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

local u273 = false

Spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1.5) do
		if _G.AutoRaidCastle then
			pcall(function()
				local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-5496.17432, 313.768921, -2841.53027, 0.924894512, 7.37058015e-09, 0.380223751, 3.5881019e-08, 1, -1.06665446e-07, -0.380223751, 1.12297109e-07, 0.924894512)
				local t16 = {
					"Galley Pirate",
					"Galley Captain",
					"Raider",
					"Mercenary",
					"Vampire",
					"Zombie",
					"Snow Trooper",
					"Winter Warrior",
					"Lab Subordinate",
					"Horned Warrior",
					"Magma Ninja",
					"Lava Pirate",
					"Ship Deckhand",
					"Ship Engineer",
					"Ship Steward",
					"Ship Officer",
					"Arctic Warrior",
					"Snow Lurker",
					"Sea Soldier",
					"Water Fighter"
				}

				if (CFrame.new(-5539.3115234375, 313.800537109375, -2972.372314453125).Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 2000 then
					u273 = true

					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and table.find(t16, child.Name) then
							if (child.HumanoidRootPart.Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 2000 then
								while true do
									task.wait()
									t9.Kill(child, _G.AutoRaidCastle)

									if _G.AutoRaidCastle and child.Parent and not (child.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
										if not workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(child.Name) then
											break
										end
									else
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				elseif not u273 then
					local v278 = false

					for i = 1, #t16 do
						if workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(t16[i]) then
							v278 = true

							break
						end
					end

					if v278 then
						_tp(cFrame3)
						u273 = true
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			u273 = false
		end
	end
end)

		end)
	end

t15.Main:AddSection("Chest")
ChestTW = t15.Main:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Farm Chest",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Farm Chest", false),
	Callback = function(p71)
		_G.AutoFarmChest = p71
		_G.SaveData["Auto Farm Chest"] = p71
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		if _G.AutoFarmChest then
			pcall(function()
				local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
				local LocalPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
				local Character = LocalPlayer2.Character or LocalPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()

				if not Character then
					return
				end

				local Position = Character:GetPivot().Position
				local tagged = CollectionService:GetTagged("_ChestTagged")
				local huge = math.huge
				local v291

				for i = 1, #tagged do
					local v293 = tagged[i]
					local Magnitude = (v293:GetPivot().Position - Position).Magnitude

					if (not SelectedIsland or v293:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland)) and not v293:GetAttribute("IsDisabled") and Magnitude < huge then
						huge = Magnitude
						v291 = v293
					end
				end

				if v291 then
					_tp(v291:GetPivot())
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
ChestBP = t15.Main:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Chest Bypass",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Chest Bypass", false),
	Callback = function(p72)
		_G.AutoChestBP = p72
		_G.SaveData["Auto Chest Bypass"] = p72
		SaveSettings()

		if p72 then
			local LocalPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
			local u296 = false
			local t17 = {}
			local u298 = true

			local function v299()
				if not LocalPlayer2.Character then
					LocalPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
				end

				LocalPlayer2.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				return LocalPlayer2.Character
			end
			local function v300()
				if u298 then
					u298 = false

					for _, descendant in pairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
						if descendant.Name:find("Chest") and descendant.ClassName == "Part" then
							table.insert(t17, descendant)
						end
					end
				end

				local t18 = {}

				for _, v in pairs(t17) do
					if v:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
						table.insert(t18, v)
					end
				end

				local LowerTorso = v299().LowerTorso

				table.sort(t18, function(p73, p74)
					return (LowerTorso.Position - p73.Position).Magnitude < (LowerTorso.Position - p74.Position).Magnitude
				end)

				return t18
			end
			local function v307()
				if u296 then
					return
				end

				u296 = true
				task.spawn(function()
					while _G.AutoChestBP and LocalPlayer2.Character and LocalPlayer2.Character.Parent do
						local v308 = v300()

						if #v308 > 0 then
							v299().HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = v308[1].CFrame
						end

						task.wait(0.1)
					end

					u296 = false
				end)
			end

			LocalPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
				v299()
				task.wait(0.5)

				if _G.AutoChestBP then
					v307()
				end
			end)
			v307()
		end
	end
})

						_G.AutoChestBP = false
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end
end)

Berry = t15.Main:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Farm Berry",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Farm Berry", false),
	Callback = function(p76)
		_G.AutoBerry = p76
		_G.SaveData["Auto Farm Berry"] = p76
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		if _G.AutoBerry then
			local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
			local LocalPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
			local tagged = CollectionService:GetTagged("BerryBush")

			for i = 1, #tagged do
				local v313 = tagged[i]

				for _, v in pairs(v313:GetAttributes()) do
					if BerryArray then
						if not table.find(BerryArray, v) then
							continue
						end
					end

					_tp(v313.Parent:GetPivot())

					for i2 = 1, #tagged do
						for _, child in pairs(tagged[i2]:GetChildren()) do
							if BerryArray then
								if not table.find(BerryArray, child) then
									continue
								end
							end

							_tp(child.WorldPivot)
							fireproximityprompt(child.ProximityPrompt, math.huge)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)
BerryH = t15.Main:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Farm Berry + Hop",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Farm Berry + Hop", false),

		_G.SaveData["Auto Farm Berry + Hop"] = p77
		SaveSettings()

spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		if _G.AutoBerryH then
			local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
			local LocalPlayer2 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
			local tagged = CollectionService:GetTagged("BerryBush")

			if #tagged == 0 then
				local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
				local t19 = {}
				local ok = pcall(function()
					t19 = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))
				end)

				if ok and t19.data then
					for _, v in pairs(t19.data) do
						if v.playing < v.maxPlayers then
							if v.id ~= game.JobId then
								TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, v.id, LocalPlayer2)

								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			else
				for i = 1, #tagged do
					local v329 = tagged[i]

					for _, v in pairs(v329:GetAttributes()) do
						if BerryArray then
							if not table.find(BerryArray, v) then
								continue
							end
						end

						_tp(v329.Parent:GetPivot())

						for i2 = 1, #tagged do
							for _, child in pairs(tagged[i2]:GetChildren()) do
								if BerryArray then
									if not table.find(BerryArray, child.Name) then
										continue
									end
								end

								_tp(child.WorldPivot)
								fireproximityprompt(child.ProximityPrompt, math.huge)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.AutoKillMob then
			pcall(function()
				if game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:FindFirstChild(getgenv().SelectMob) then
					for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == getgenv().SelectMob and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
							while true do
								game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Wait()
								t9.Kill(child, _G.AutoKillMob)

								if _G.AutoKillMob then
									if not child.Parent then
										break
									end

									if child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end

									continue
								end

								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

local v340

	v340 = t20

	v340 = t21

t15.Main:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Farm All Island",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Farm All Island", false),
	Callback = function(p85)
		_G.AutoFarmIsland = p85
		_G.SaveData["Auto Farm All Island"] = p85
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.2) do
		if _G.AutoFarmIsland then
			if _G.SelectIsland then
				if v340 then
					local v341 = v340[_G.SelectIsland]

					if v341 then
						local CFrame_ = v341.CFrame
						local t23 = {}

						for _, mob in ipairs(v341.Mobs) do
							t23[mob] = true
						end

						local v346 = false

						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if t23[child.Name] and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
								if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
									v346 = true

									while true do
										task.wait()

											end
										else
											break
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if not v346 then
							_tp(CFrame_)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

local function v365()
	local HttpService2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
	local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
	local s11 = ""
	local v369 = false

	repeat
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100&cursor=" .. s11)
		end)

		if ok and result then
			local data = HttpService2:JSONDecode(result)

			if data.data then
				for _, v in pairs(data.data) do
					if v.playing < v.maxPlayers and v.id ~= game.JobId then
						v369 = true
						TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, v.id)

						break
					end
				end

				s11 = data.nextPageCursor or ""
			end
		end
	until not s11 or v369
end

AuraSkin = function(p90)
	local t24 = { {
		StorageName = p90,
		Type = "AuraSkin",
		Context = "Equip"
	} }

	replicated:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RF/FruitCustomizerRF"):InvokeServer(unpack(t24))
end
VaildColor = function(p91)
	if p91 and p91.BrickColor then
		return tostring(p91.BrickColor) == "Lime green"
	end
end
HakiCalculate = function(p92)
	local t25 = {
		["Really red"] = "Pure Red",
		Oyster = "Snow White",
		["Hot pink"] = "Winter Sky"
	}

	if p92 and p92.BrickColor then
		return t25[tostring(p92.BrickColor)]
	end
end
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		if _G.AutoUnHaki then
			pcall(function()
				local Summoner = workspace.Map["Boat Castle"]:FindFirstChild("Summoner")

				if Summoner and Summoner:FindFirstChild("Circle") then
					local pairs_ = pairs
					local Circle = Summoner:FindFirstChild("Circle")

					for _, child in pairs_(Circle:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "Part" then
							local Part2 = child:FindFirstChild("Part")

							if VaildColor(Part2) == false then
								AuraSkin(HakiCalculate(child))

								while true do
									wait()
									_tp(child.CFrame)

									if VaildColor(Part2) == true then
										break
									elseif not _G.AutoUnHaki then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

local function v412()
	pcall(function()
		local HttpService2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local t27 = {}
		local response = game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")

		for _, v in pairs(HttpService2:JSONDecode(response).data) do
			if v.playing < v.maxPlayers then
				table.insert(t27, v.id)
			end
		end

		if #t27 > 0 then
			game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, t27[math.random(1, #t27)], game.Players.LocalPlayer)
		end
	end)
end

							game.ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(unpack(t28[math.random(1, #t28)]))
						end

						return
					end

					local v426
					local huge = math.huge
					local Enemies_ = workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies")
					local t29 = {
						"Reborn Skeleton",
						"Living Zombie",
						"Demonic Soul",
						"Possessed Mummy"
					}

					if Enemies_ then
						local children = Enemies_:GetChildren()

						for i = 1, #children do
							local v432 = children[i]
							local v433 = false

							for i2 = 1, #t29 do
								if v432.Name == t29[i2] then
									v433 = true

									break
								end
							end

							if v433 and v432:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v432.Humanoid.Health > 0 and v432:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
								local Magnitude = (v432.HumanoidRootPart.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

								if Magnitude < huge then
									huge = Magnitude
									v426 = v432
								end
							end
						end
					end

					if v426 then
						_tp(v426.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))

						while true do
							task.wait()
							getgenv().OnFarm = true
							shouldTween = true
							t9.Kill(v426, true)

							if _G.AutoFarm_Bone then
								if not v426.Parent or v426.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end
					else
						local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-9495.68066, 453.58624, 5977.34863)

						_tp(cFrame3)

						while true do
							if _G.AutoFarm_Bone then
								if not workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChildOfClass("Model") and (cFrame3.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 50 then
									task.wait(0.2)

									continue
								end

								break
							end

							break
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)

	Check_Eye = function()
		local IslandModel = workspace.Map.TikiOutpost.IslandModel
		local n3 = 0

		for _, v in ipairs({
			IslandModel.Eye1,
			IslandModel.Eye2,
			IslandModel.IslandChunks.E.Eye3,
			IslandModel.IslandChunks.E.Eye4
		}) do
			if v and v.Transparency ~= 1 then
				n3 += 1
			end
		end

		return n3, n3 == 4
	end
	task.spawn(function()
		local v446 = false

		while task.wait(1) do

				v446 = true

local function v457(p107)
	local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

	VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, p107, false, game)
	wait(0.05)
	VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, p107, false, game)
end
local function v459(p108)
	if not plr.Character or not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or not (plr.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
		return
	end

	for _, child in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") and child.ToolTip == p108 then
			child.Parent = plr.Character
			wait(0.12)

			for _, v in ipairs({
				"Z",
				"X",
				"C",
				"V",
				"F"
			}) do
				if not _G.FarmPhaBinh then
					break
				end

				pcall(function()
					v457(v)
				end)
				wait(0.12)
			end

			child.Parent = plr.Backpack

			return
		end
	end
end

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.3) do
		if _G.AutoFarmAllBoss then
			pcall(function()
				local LocalPlayer2 = game.Players.LocalPlayer

				if LocalPlayer2.Character then
					if LocalPlayer2.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer2.Character.HumanoidRootPart
						local v488
						local huge = math.huge

						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and table.find(BossList, child.Name) then
								local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

								if Magnitude < huge then
									v488 = child
									huge = Magnitude
								end
							end
						end

						if v488 and v488:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							local HumanoidRootPart2 = v488.HumanoidRootPart
							local Humanoid = v488.Humanoid

							repeat
								task.wait(0.1)

								if not _G.AutoFarmAllBoss then
									break
								end

								local v495 = HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)

								if (HumanoidRootPart.Position - v495.Position).Magnitude > 100 then
									LocalPlayer2.Character:PivotTo(v495)
								else
									_tp(v495)
								end

								if t9 and typeof(t9.Kill) == "function" then
									t9.Kill(v488, true)
								end
							until not v488.Parent or Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not _G.AutoFarmAllBoss
						end

						return
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

								elseif v499.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v499.Parent then
									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-9495.6806640625, 453.58624267578125, 5977.3486328125))
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
	t15.Main:AddToggle({
		Name = "Auto Mastery Gun",
		Description = "",
		Default = GetSetting("Auto Mastery Gun", false),
		Callback = function(p119)
			_G.FarmMastery_G = p119
			_G.SaveData["Auto Mastery Gun"] = p119
			SaveSettings()
		end
	})
	spawn(function()
		while wait(1.5) do
			if _G.FarmMastery_G then
				pcall(function()
					if SelectIsland == "Cake" then
						local v500 = GetConnectionEnemies(t5)

						if v500 then
							HealthM = v500.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 70 / 100

							repeat
								wait()
								MousePos = v500.HumanoidRootPart.Position
								t9.Masgun(v500, _G.FarmMastery_G)

								local REShootGunEvent = replicated:FindFirstChild("Modules"):FindFirstChild("Net"):FindFirstChild("RE/ShootGunEvent")

								if plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").ToolTip ~= "Gun" then
									return
								end

								local Character, Character2

								if plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
									if plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").Name == "Skull Guitar" then
										SoulGuitar = true
										plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").RemoteEvent:FireServer("TAP", MousePos)

										if _G.FarmMastery_G then
											vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
											wait(0.05)
											vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
											wait(0.05)
										end
									else
										Character = plr.Character

										if Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
											Character2 = plr.Character

											if Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").Name ~= "Skull Guitar" then
												SoulGuitar = false
												REShootGunEvent:FireServer(MousePos, { v500.HumanoidRootPart })

												if _G.FarmMastery_G then
													vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
													wait(0.05)
													vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
													wait(0.05)
												end
											end
										end
									end
								else
									Character = plr.Character

									if Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
										Character2 = plr.Character

										if Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").Name ~= "Skull Guitar" then
											SoulGuitar = false
											REShootGunEvent:FireServer(MousePos, { v500.HumanoidRootPart })

											if _G.FarmMastery_G then
												vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
												wait(0.05)
												vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
												wait(0.05)
											end
										end
									end

									if _G.FarmMastery_G == false or v500.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v500.Parent then
										break
									end
								end
							until _G.FarmMastery_G == false or v500.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v500.Parent

							SoulGuitar = false
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-1943.676513671875, 251.50956726074219, -12337.880859375))
						end
					elseif SelectIsland == "Bone" then
						local v504 = GetConnectionEnemies(t6)

						if v504 then
							HealthM = v504.Humanoid.MaxHealth * 70 / 100

							repeat
								wait()
								MousePos = v504.HumanoidRootPart.Position
								t9.Masgun(v504, _G.FarmMastery_G)

								local REShootGunEvent = replicated:FindFirstChild("Modules"):FindFirstChild("Net"):FindFirstChild("RE/ShootGunEvent")

								if plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").ToolTip ~= "Gun" then
									return
								end

								local Character, Character2

								if plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
									if plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").Name == "Skull Guitar" then
										SoulGuitar = true
										plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").RemoteEvent:FireServer("TAP", MousePos)

										if _G.FarmMastery_G then
											vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
											wait(0.05)
											vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
											wait(0.05)
										end
									else
										Character = plr.Character

										if Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
											Character2 = plr.Character

											if Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").Name ~= "Skull Guitar" then
												SoulGuitar = false
												REShootGunEvent:FireServer(MousePos, { v504.HumanoidRootPart })

												if _G.FarmMastery_G then
													vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
													wait(0.05)
													vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
													wait(0.05)
												end
											end
										end
									end
								else
									Character = plr.Character

									if Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
										Character2 = plr.Character

										if Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").Name ~= "Skull Guitar" then
											SoulGuitar = false
											REShootGunEvent:FireServer(MousePos, { v504.HumanoidRootPart })

											if _G.FarmMastery_G then
												vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
												wait(0.05)
												vim1:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
												wait(0.05)
											end
										end
									end

									if _G.FarmMastery_G == false or v504.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v504.Parent then
										break
									end
								end
							until _G.FarmMastery_G == false or v504.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v504.Parent

							SoulGuitar = false
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-9495.6806640625, 453.58624267578125, 5977.3486328125))
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

t15.Main:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Mastery All Sword",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Mastery All Sword", false),
	Callback = function(p120)
		_G.FarmMastery_S = p120
		_G.SaveData["Auto Mastery All Sword"] = p120
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.FarmMastery_S then
				if SelectIsland == "Cake" then
					local next_ = next
					local response, v510 = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
					local exitTo2
					local v512

					for _, v in next_, response, v510 do
						if type(v) == "table" and v.Type == "Sword" then
							v512 = v
							exitTo2 = 1

							break
						end
					end

					if exitTo2 == 1 then
						SwordName = v512.Name

						if tonumber(v512.Mastery) >= 1 or tonumber(v512.Mastery) <= 599 then
							local v515 = GetConnectionEnemies(t5)

							if GetBP(SwordName) then
								if v515 then
									while true do
										wait()
										t9.Sword(v515, _G.FarmMastery_S)

										if _G.FarmMastery_S == false then
											break
										elseif not v515.Parent or v515.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
											break
										end
									end
								else
									_tp(CFrame.new(-1943.676513671875, 251.50956726074219, -12337.880859375))
								end
							else
								replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", SwordName)
							end
						elseif tonumber(v512.Mastery) >= 600 then
							if GetBP(SwordName) then
								return nil
							end

							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", SwordName)
						end
					end
				elseif SelectIsland == "Bone" then
					local next_ = next
					local response, v518 = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
					local exitTo
					local v520

					for _, v in next_, response, v518 do
						if type(v) == "table" and v.Type == "Sword" then
							v520 = v
							exitTo = 1

							break
						end
					end

					if exitTo == 1 then
						SwordName = v520.Name

						if tonumber(v520.Mastery) >= 1 or tonumber(v520.Mastery) <= 599 then
							local v523 = GetConnectionEnemies(t6)

							if GetBP(SwordName) then
								if v523 then
									while true do
										wait()
										t9.Sword(v523, _G.FarmMastery_S)

										if _G.FarmMastery_S == false then
											break
										elseif not v523.Parent or v523.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
											break
										end
									end
								else
									_tp(CFrame.new(-9495.6806640625, 453.58624267578125, 5977.3486328125))
								end
							else
								replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", SwordName)
							end
						elseif tonumber(v520.Mastery) >= 600 then
							if GetBP(SwordName) then
								return nil
							end

							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", SwordName)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

t15.Settings:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Hop Server with time",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Hop Server with time", false),
	Callback = function(p127)
		_G.AutoHopServer = p127

		if not p127 then
			_G.HopTimer = nil
			_G.SaveData["Auto Hop Server with time"] = p127
			SaveSettings()
		end
	end
})
Spawn(function()
	while Wait(1) do
		if _G.AutoHopServer then
			pcall(function()
				if not _G.HopTimer then
					_G.HopTimer = tick()
				end

				if _G.HopDelay <= tick() - _G.HopTimer then
					_G.HopTimer = tick()

					if syn and syn.queue_on_teleport then
						syn.queue_on_teleport("loadstring(game:HttpGet('https://pastefy.app/iiFOhcot/raw'))()")
					end

					game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

	Callback = function(p141)
		getgenv().NoClip = p141
	end
})
spawn(function()
	pcall(function()
		game:GetService("RunService").Stepped:Connect(function()
			if getgenv().NoClip then
				for _, descendant in pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
					if not descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						if not descendant:IsA("Part") then

				end
			end
		end)
	end)

FishingToggle = t15.Fish:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Fishing",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Fishing", false),

		_G.AutoFishing = p151
		_G.SaveData["Auto Fishing"] = p151
		SaveSettings()

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer2 = Players.LocalPlayer
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local ReplicatedStorage2 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local FishReplicated = ReplicatedStorage2:WaitForChild("FishReplicated")
local FishingRequest = FishReplicated:WaitForChild("FishingRequest")
local Config = require(FishReplicated.FishingClient.Config)
local GetWaterHeightAtLocation = require(ReplicatedStorage2.Util.GetWaterHeightAtLocation)
local MaxLaunchDistance = Config.Rod.MaxLaunchDistance

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.5) do
		if _G.AutoFishing then
			pcall(function()
				local Character = LocalPlayer2.Character or LocalPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()
				local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not HumanoidRootPart then
					return
				end

				local Tool = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

				if _G.SelectedRod and (not Tool or Tool.Name ~= _G.SelectedRod) then
					local _GSelectedRod = LocalPlayer2.Backpack:FindFirstChild(_G.SelectedRod)

					if _GSelectedRod then
						Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(_GSelectedRod)
						Tool = _GSelectedRod
					end
				end

				if Tool then
					local v546 = GetWaterHeightAtLocation(HumanoidRootPart.Position)
					local _, v548 = Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(Character.Head.Position, HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector * MaxLaunchDistance), {
						Character,
						Workspace.Characters,
						Workspace.Enemies
					})
					local vector3 = v548 and Vector3.new(v548.X, math.max(v548.Y, v546), v548.Z)
					local attribute = Tool:GetAttribute("State")
					local attribute2 = Tool:GetAttribute("ServerState")

					if vector3 and (attribute == "ReeledIn" or attribute2 == "ReeledIn") then
						FishingRequest:InvokeServer("StartCasting")
						task.wait()
						FishingRequest:InvokeServer("CastLineAtLocation", vector3, 100, true)
					elseif attribute2 == "Biting" then
						FishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catching", true)
						task.wait(0.1)
						FishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catch", 1)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

local LocalPlayer3 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local RFJobsRemoteFunction = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Net:WaitForChild("RF/JobsRemoteFunction")

local function v554()
	local Quest = LocalPlayer3.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Quest") or LocalPlayer3.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("QuestGui")

	if Quest and Quest:FindFirstChild("Container") then
		if Quest.Container:FindFirstChild("QuestTitle") then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1) do
		if _G.AutoFishingQuest then
			pcall(function()
				if not v554() then
					RFJobsRemoteFunction:InvokeServer("FishingNPC", "Angler", "AskQuest")
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

TravelDress = t15.Quests:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Quest Sea 2",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Quest Sea 2", false),
	Callback = function(p156)
		_G.TravelDres = p156
		_G.SaveData["Auto Quest Sea 2"] = p156
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.TravelDres then
				if plr.Data.Level.Value >= 700 then
					if workspace.Map.Ice.Door.CanCollide == true and workspace.Map.Ice.Door.Transparency == 0 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("DressrosaQuestProgress", "Detective")
						EquipWeapon("Key")

						while true do
							wait()
							_tp(CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488))

							if _G.TravelDres then
								if Root.Position == CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488).Position then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end
					elseif workspace.Map.Ice.Door.CanCollide ~= false or workspace.Map.Ice.Door.Transparency ~= 1 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelDressrosa")
					elseif Enemies:FindFirstChild("Ice Admiral") then
						for _, child in pairs(Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "Ice Admiral" then
								if t9.Alive(child) then
									while true do
										task.wait()
										t9.Kill(child, _G.TravelDres)

										if _G.TravelDres == false then
											break
										elseif child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
											break
										end
									end

									replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelDressrosa")
								end
							end
						end
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(1347.7124, 37.3751602, -1325.6488))
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
Zou = t15.Quests:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Quest Sea 3",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Quest Sea 3", false),
	Callback = function(p157)
		_G.AutoZou = p157
		_G.SaveData["Auto Quest Sea 3"] = p157
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.AutoZou and plr.Data.Level.Value >= 1500 then
				if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BartiloQuestProgress", "Bartilo") ~= 3 then
					if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BartiloQuestProgress", "Bartilo") == 0 then
						if not string.find(plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, "Swan Pirates") then
							_tp(CFrame.new(-456.28952, 73.0200958, 299.895966))

							return
						end

						if string.find(plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, "50") and plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == true then
							local v559 = GetConnectionEnemies("Swan Pirate")

							if v559 then
								pcall(function()
									while true do
										wait()
										t9.Kill(v559, _G.AutoZou)

										if not v559.Parent then
											break
										end

										if not (v559.Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
											if _G.AutoZou ~= false then
												if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible ~= false then
													continue
												end
											end
										end

										break
									end
								end)
							else
								_tp(CFrame.new(1057.92761, 137.614319, 1242.08069))
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-456.28952, 73.0200958, 299.895966))
						end
					elseif replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BartiloQuestProgress", "Bartilo") == 1 then
						local Jeremy = GetConnectionEnemies("Jeremy")

						if Jeremy then
							while true do
								wait()
								t9.Kill(Jeremy, _G.AutoZou)

								if Jeremy.Parent then
									if Jeremy.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or _G.AutoZou == false then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(2099.88159, 448.931, 648.997375))
						end
					elseif replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BartiloQuestProgress", "Bartilo") == 2 then
						while true do
							wait()
							_tp(CFrame.new(-1836, 11, 1714))

							if _G.AutoZou then
								if Root.Position == CFrame.new(-1836, 11, 1714).Position then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end

						if Root.CFrame == CFrame.new(-1836, 11, 1714) then
							notween(CFrame.new(-1836, 11, 1714))
						end

						notween(CFrame.new(-1850.49329, 13.1789551, 1750.89685))
						wait(0.1)
						notween(CFrame.new(-1858.87305, 19.3777466, 1712.01807))
						wait(0.1)
						notween(CFrame.new(-1803.94324, 16.5789185, 1750.89685))
						wait(0.1)
						notween(CFrame.new(-1858.55835, 16.8604317, 1724.79541))
						wait(0.1)
						notween(CFrame.new(-1869.54224, 15.987854, 1681.00659))
						wait(0.1)
						notween(CFrame.new(-1800.0979, 16.4978027, 1684.52368))
						wait(0.1)
						notween(CFrame.new(-1819.26343, 14.795166, 1717.90625))
						wait(0.1)
						notween(CFrame.new(-1813.51843, 14.8604736, 1724.79541))
					end
				elseif replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil then
					if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil then
						TabelDevilFruitStore = {}
						TabelDevilFruitOpen = {}

						for _, v in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventoryFruits")) do
							for k, v2 in pairs(v) do
								if k == "Name" then
									table.insert(TabelDevilFruitStore, v2)
								end
							end
						end

						local next_ = next
						local response, v567 = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes").CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")

						for _, v in next_, response, v567 do
							if v.Price >= 1000000 then
								table.insert(TabelDevilFruitOpen, v.Name)
							end
						end

						for _, v in pairs(TabelDevilFruitOpen) do
							for _, v2 in pairs(TabelDevilFruitStore) do
								if v == v2 then
									if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil then
										if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(v2) then
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "1")
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "2")
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "3")
										else
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "LoadFruit", v2)
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "1")
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "2")
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TalkTrevor", "3")
					end
				else
					replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TravelZou")

					if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("ZQuestProgress", "Check") == 0 then
						local ripIndra = GetConnectionEnemies("rip_indra")

						if ripIndra then
							while true do
								wait()
								t9.Kill(ripIndra, _G.AutoZou)

								if _G.AutoZou then
									if not ripIndra.Parent or ripIndra.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end

							Check = 2

							repeat
								wait()
								replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TravelZou")
							until Check == 1
						else
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "ZQuestProgress", "Check")
							wait(0.1)
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "ZQuestProgress", "Begin")
						end
					elseif replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("ZQuestProgress", "Check") == 1 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("F_", "TravelZou")
					else
						local v575 = GetConnectionEnemies("Don Swan")

						if v575 then
							while true do
								wait()
								t9.Kill(v575, _G.AutoZou)

								if _G.AutoZou then
									if not v575.Parent or v575.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end
						else
							while true do
								wait()
								_tp(CFrame.new(2288.802, 15.1870775, 863.034607))

								if _G.AutoZou then
									if Root.Position == CFrame.new(2288.802, 15.1870775, 863.034607).Position then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end

							if Root.CFrame == CFrame.new(2288.802, 15.1870775, 863.034607) then
								notween(CFrame.new(2288.802, 15.1870775, 863.034607))
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

getT = function(p161)
	local Rotation

	if p161 == 1 then
		Rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment1.Line.Rotation
	elseif p161 == 3 then
		Rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment3.Line.Rotation
	elseif p161 == 4 then
		Rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment4.Line.Rotation
	elseif p161 == 7 then
		Rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment7.Line.Rotation
	elseif p161 == 10 then
		Rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment10.Line.Rotation
	end

	if Rotation then
		return Rotation.Z
	end
end
getRT = function(p162)
	local Rotation

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Trophies.Quest:GetChildren()) do
		if p162 == 1 and child.Name == "Trophy1" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
			Rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
		elseif p162 == 2 and child.Name == "Trophy2" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
			Rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
		elseif p162 == 3 and child.Name == "Trophy3" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
			Rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
		elseif p162 == 4 and child.Name == "Trophy4" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
			Rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
		elseif p162 == 5 and child.Name == "Trophy5" and child:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
			Rotation = child.Handle.Rotation
		end

		if Rotation then
			return Rotation.Z
		end
	end
end
GetFirePlacard = function(p163, p164)
	if tostring(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"]["Placard" .. p163][p164].Indicator.BrickColor) ~= "Pearl" then
		fireclickdetector(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"]["Placard" .. p163][p164].ClickDetector)
	end
end

							"Ship Deckhand",
							"Ship Engineer",

							"Arctic Warrior"

							while true do
								task.wait()

										break

									break
								end
							end

							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("requestEntrance", Vector3.new(923.21252441406, 126.9760055542, 32852.83203125))
						end
					else
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelDressrosa")
					end
				elseif GetM("Dark Fragment") < 1 then
					if _G.AutoMatSoul and World2 then
						local Darkbeard = GetConnectionEnemies("Darkbeard")

						if Darkbeard then
							while true do
								task.wait()

								if _G.AutoMatSoul then
									break

									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(3798.4575195313, 13.826690673828, -3399.806640625))
						end
					else
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelDressrosa")
					end

					if not GetConnectionEnemies("Darkbeard") then
						Hop()
					end
				elseif GetM("Bones") <= 500 then
					if _G.AutoMatSoul and World3 then
						local v597 = GetConnectionEnemies({
							"Reborn Skeleton",
							"Living Zombie",
							"Demonic Soul",
							"Posessed Mummy"
						})

						if v597 then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								t9.Kill(v597, _G.AutoMatSoul)

								if _G.AutoMatSoul then
									if v597.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v597.Parent or v597.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-9504.8564453125, 172.14292907714844, 6057.259765625))
						end
					else
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelZou")
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

										if ok3 then
											for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
												if (child.Name == "Reborn Skeleton" or child.Name == "Living Zombie" or child.Name == "Demonic Soul" or child.Name == "Posessed Mummy") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid").Health > 0 then
													while true do
														task.wait()
														t9.Kill(child, _G.CDK_YM)

														if _G.CDK_YM then
															if child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
																break
															end

															if not child.Parent then
																break
															end
														else
															break
														end
													end
												end
											end
										else
											_tp(CFrame.new(-9515.2255859375, 164.0062255859375, 5785.38330078125))
										end
									else
										replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Buy", 1, 1)
									end
								else
									_tp(replicated:FindFirstChild("Soul Reaper").HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

Q = t15.Quests:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Unlocked DonSwan",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Unlocked DonSwan", false),
	Callback = function(p188)
		_G.Auto_DonAcces = p188
		_G.SaveData["Auto Unlocked DonSwan"] = p188
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(0.1) do
		if _G.Auto_DonAcces then
			pcall(function()
				if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil and plr.Data.Level.Value >= 1500 then
					FruitPrice = {}
					FruitStore = {}

					local next_ = next
					local response, v668 = replicated:WaitForChild("Remotes").CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")

					for _, v in next_, response, v668 do
						if v.Price >= 1000000 then
							table.insert(FruitPrice, v.Name)
						end
					end

					for _, v in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventoryFruits")) do
						for k, v2 in pairs(v) do
							if k == "Name" then
								table.insert(FruitStore, v2)
							end
						end

						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Cousin", "Buy")

						for _, v2 in pairs(FruitPrice) do
							for _, v3 in pairs(FruitStore) do
								if v2 == v3 then
									if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil then
										_G.StoreF = false

										if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(FruitStore) then
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor", "1")
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor", "2")
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor", "3")
										else
											replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadFruit", tostring(v2))
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess ~= nil then
							_G.StoreF = true
							_G.Auto_DonAcces = false
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
