						while true do
							wait()
							_tp(CFrame.new(-461.533203, 72.3478546, 300.311096, 0.050853312, -0, -0.998706102, 0, 1, -0, 0.998706102, 0, 0.050853312))

							if (CFrame.new(-461.533203, 72.3478546, 300.311096, 0.050853312, -0, -0.998706102, 0, 1, -0, 0.998706102, 0, 0.050853312).Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 20 then
								break
							elseif _G.Bartilo_Quest == false then
								break
							end
						end

						if (CFrame.new(-461.533203, 72.3478546, 300.311096, 0.050853312, -0, -0.998706102, 0, 1, -0, 0.998706102, 0, 0.050853312).Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", "BartiloQuest", 1)
						end
					elseif GetConnectionEnemies("Swan Pirate") then
						local v734 = GetConnectionEnemies(t3)

						if v734 then
							repeat
								task.wait()

								if string.find(plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, "Swan Pirate") then
									t9.Kill(v734, _G.Bartilo_Quest)
								else
									replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
								end
							until _G.Bartilo_Quest == false or not v734.Parent or v734.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or Quest.Visible == false or not v734:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						end
					else
						_tp(CFrame.nee(970.369446, 142.653198, 1217.3667, 0.162079468, -4.85452638e-08, -0.986777723, 1.03357589e-08, 1, -4.74980872e-08, 0.986777723, -2.50063148e-09, 0.162079468))
					end
				elseif replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BartiloQuestProgress", "Bartilo") == 1 then
					_G.Level = false

					local Jeremy = GetConnectionEnemies("Jeremy")

					if Jeremy then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							t9.Kill(Jeremy, _G.Bartilo_Quest)

							if _G.Bartilo_Quest == false then
								break
							elseif not Jeremy.Parent or Jeremy.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or Quest.Visible == false or not Jeremy:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
								break
							end
						end
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(2158.97412, 449.056244, 705.411682, -0.754199564, -4.17389057e-09, -0.656645238, -4.47752875e-08, 1, 4.50709301e-08, 0.656645238, 6.3393955e-08, -0.754199564))
					end
				elseif replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BartiloQuestProgress", "Bartilo") == 2 then
					while true do
						wait()
						_tp(CFrame.new(-1830.83972, 10.5578213, 1680.60229, 0.979988456, -2.02152783e-08, -0.199054286, 2.20792113e-08, 1, 7.1442483e-09, 0.199054286, -1.13962431e-08, 0.979988456))

						if (CFrame.new(-1830.83972, 10.5578213, 1680.60229, 0.979988456, -2.02152783e-08, -0.199054286, 2.20792113e-08, 1, 7.1442483e-09, 0.199054286, -1.13962431e-08, 0.979988456).Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 1 then
							break
						elseif _G.Bartilo_Quest == false then
							break
						end
					end

					wait(0.5)
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map.Dressrosa.BartiloPlates.Plate1.CFrame
					wait(0.5)
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map.Dressrosa.BartiloPlates.Plate2.CFrame
					wait(0.5)
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map.Dressrosa.BartiloPlates.Plate3.CFrame
					wait(0.5)
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map.Dressrosa.BartiloPlates.Plate4.CFrame
					wait(0.5)
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map.Dressrosa.BartiloPlates.Plate5.CFrame
					wait(0.5)
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map.Dressrosa.BartiloPlates.Plate6.CFrame
					wait(0.5)

					wait(0.5)
					plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.Map.Dressrosa.BartiloPlates.Plate8.CFrame

CitizenQ = t15.Quests:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Done Citizen Quest",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Done Citizen Quest", false),
	Callback = function(p201)
		_G.CitizenQuest = p201
		_G.SaveData["Auto Done Citizen Quest"] = p201
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.CitizenQuest then
				local ok2 = not (Lv >= 1800)

				if not ok2 then
					ok2 = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CitizenQuestProgress").KilledBandits ~= false
				end

				if ok2 then
					local ok = not (Lv >= 1800)

					if not ok then
						ok = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CitizenQuestProgress").KilledBoss ~= false
					end

					if not ok then
						local v738 = GetConnectionEnemies("Captain Elephant")

						if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible and string.find(plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, "Captain Elephant") and plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == true then
							if v738 then
								while true do
									task.wait()
									t9.Kill(v738, _G.CitizenQuest)

									if _G.CitizenQuest == false then
										break
									elseif v738.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v738.Parent or plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false then
										break
									end
								end
							else
								_tp(CFrame.new(-13374.889648438, 421.27752685547, -8225.208984375))
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-12443.8671875, 332.40396118164, -7675.4892578125))

							if (CFrame.new(-12443.8671875, 332.40396118164, -7675.4892578125).Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 4 then
								wait(1.5)
								replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CitizenQuestProgress", "Citizen")
							end
						end
					elseif Lv >= 1800 then
						if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CitizenQuestProgress", "Citizen") == 2 then
							_tp(CFrame.new(-12512.138671875, 340.39279174805, -9872.8203125))
						end
					end
				elseif string.find(plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, "Forest Pirate") and string.find(plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, "50") and plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == true then
					local v739 = GetConnectionEnemies("Forest Pirate")

					if v739 then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							t9.Kill(v739, _G.CitizenQuest)

							if _G.CitizenQuest == false then
								break
							elseif not v739.Parent or v739.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false then
								break
							end
						end
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(-13206.452148438, 425.89199829102, -7964.5537109375))
					end
				else
					_tp(CFrame.new(-12443.8671875, 332.40396118164, -7675.4892578125))

					if (Vector3.new(-12443.8671875, 332.40396118164, -7675.4892578125) - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
						wait(1.5)
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("StartQuest", "CitizenQuest", 1)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

									t9.Kill(black, _G.Snaguine)

									if _G.Snaguine then
										break
									elseif black.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not black.Parent then
										break
									end
								end
							else
								_tp(CFrame.new(3798.4575195313, 13.826690673828, -3399.806640625))
							end
						else
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelDressrosa")
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

t15.Race:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Find Mirage Island",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Find Mirage Island", false),
	Callback = function(p210)
		_G.FindMirage = p210
		_G.SaveData["Auto Find Mirage Island"] = p210
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.FindMirage then
			pcall(function()
				if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Mirage Island", true) then
					_tp(workspace.Map.MysticIsland.Center.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 300, 0))
				else
					local v750 = CheckBoat()

					if not v750 then
						local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)

						TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

						if (cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat)
						end
					elseif plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
						_tp(v750.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))
					else
						while true do
							wait()

							local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)

							if CheckEnemiesBoat() or CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
								_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 150, 37016.25))
							else
								_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25))
							end

							if _G.FindMirage and not ((cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10) then
								if not workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Mirage Island") and plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit ~= false then
									continue
								end
							end

							break
						end

						plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

t15.Race:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Collect Mirage Chest",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Collect Mirage Chest", false),
	Callback = function(p217)
		_G.FarmChestM = p217
		_G.SaveData["Auto Collect Mirage Chest"] = p217
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(0.2) do
		if _G.FarmChestM then
			pcall(function()
				local ok = workspace.Map.MysticIsland.Chests:FindFirstChild("DiamondChest")

				if not ok then
					ok = not not workspace.Map.MysticIsland.Chests:FindFirstChild("FragChest")
				end

				if ok then
					local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")
					local LocalPlayer4 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
					local Character = LocalPlayer4.Character or LocalPlayer4.CharacterAdded:Wait()

					if not Character then
						return
					end

					local Position = Character:GetPivot().Position
					local tagged = CollectionService:GetTagged("_ChestTagged")
					local huge = math.huge
					local v772

					for i = 1, #tagged do
						local v774 = tagged[i]
						local Magnitude = (v774:GetPivot().Position - Position).Magnitude

						if (not SelectedIsland or v774:IsDescendantOf(SelectedIsland)) and not v774:GetAttribute("IsDisabled") and Magnitude < huge then
							huge = Magnitude
							v772 = v774
						end
					end

					if v772 then
						_tp(v772:GetPivot())
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.AcientOne then
				local t33 = {
					"Reborn Skeleton",
					"Living Zombie",
					"Demonic Soul",
					"Posessed Mummy"
				}

				for i = 1, #t33 do
					if plr.Character:FindFirstChild("RaceEnergy").Value == 1 then
						vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Y", false, game)
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("UpgradeRace", "Buy")
						_tp(CFrame.new(-8987.041015625, 215.862060546875, 5886.71044921875))
					elseif plr.Character:FindFirstChild("RaceTransformed").Value == false then
						local v799 = GetConnectionEnemies(t33)

						if v799 then
							while true do
								wait()
								t9.Kill(v799, _G.AcientOne)

								if _G.AcientOne == false then
									break
								elseif v799.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not v799.Parent then
									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-9495.6806640625, 453.58624267578125, 5977.3486328125))
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
t15.Race:AddButton({
	Name = "Teleport to Temple of Time",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		local LocalPlayer4 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
		local HumanoidRootPart = LocalPlayer4.Character and LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if HumanoidRootPart then
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(28286.35546875, 14895.3017578125, 102.62469482421875)
		end

		if not game:GetService("Workspace").Map:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") and World3 then
			local MapStash = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("MapStash")

			if MapStash and MapStash:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") then
				MapStash["Temple of Time"].Parent = workspace.Map
			end
		end
	end
})

	end
})

	Description = "",
	Callback = function()

		local cFrame4 = CFrame.new(29549, 15069, -88)

		if HumanoidRootPart then
			HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame3
		end

		task.delay(2, function()
			_tp(cFrame4)
		end)

		if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") and World3 then
			local MapStash = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("MapStash")

			if MapStash and MapStash:FindFirstChild("Temple of Time") then
				MapStash["Temple of Time"].Parent = workspace.Map
			end
		end
	end
})

Trials = t15.Race:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Complete Trial Race",

GetSeaBeastTrial = function()
	if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("FishmanTrial") then
		return nil
	end

	if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water") then
		FishmanTrial = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water")
	end

	if FishmanTrial then
		local next_ = next
		local children, v813 = workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()

		for _, v in next_, children, v813 do
			if v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (v.HumanoidRootPart.Position - FishmanTrial.Position).Magnitude <= 1500 and v.Health.Value > 0 then
				return v
			end
		end
	end
end
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Complete_Trials then
				if tostring(plr.Data.Race.Value) == "Mink" then
					notween(workspace.Map.MinkTrial.Ceiling.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -20, 0))
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Complete_Trials then
				if tostring(plr.Data.Race.Value) == "Fishman" and GetSeaBeastTrial() then
					while true do
						task.wait()
						spawn(function()
							_tp(CFrame.new(GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, game:GetService("Workspace").Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 300, GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
						end)
						MousePos = GetSeaBeastTrial().HumanoidRootPart.Position
						Useskills("Melee", "Z")
						Useskills("Melee", "X")
						Useskills("Melee", "C")
						wait(0.1)
						Useskills("Sword", "Z")
						Useskills("Sword", "X")
						wait(0.1)
						Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
						Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
						Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
						wait(0.1)
						Useskills("Gun", "Z")
						Useskills("Gun", "X")

						if _G.Complete_Trials == false then
							break
						elseif not GetSeaBeastTrial() then
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Complete_Trials then
				if tostring(plr.Data.Race.Value) == "Cyborg" then

end)

	end
end)
spawn(function()

			if _G.Complete_Trials then
				if tostring(plr.Data.Race.Value) == "Human" or tostring(plr.Data.Race.Value) == "Ghoul" then

						"Ancient Zombie"
					})

							end

					end

			end

end)
AutoKill = t15.Race:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Kill Player After Trial",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Kill Player After Trial", false),
	Callback = function(p228)
		_G.Defeating = p228
		_G.SaveData["Auto Kill Player After Trial"] = p228
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Defeating then
				for _, child in pairs(workspace.Characters:GetChildren()) do
					if child.Name ~= plr.Name and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child.Parent and (Root.Position - child.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 250 then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
							_tp(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 15))
							sethiddenproperty(plr, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)

							if _G.Defeating == false then
								break
							elseif child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not child.Parent or not child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or not child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

printBeltName = function(p230)
	if type(p230) == "table" and p230.Quest.BeltName then
		return p230.Quest.BeltName
	end
end
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		if _G.Dojoo then
			pcall(function()
				local response = replicated.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest"):InvokeServer(unpack({ {
					NPC = "Dojo Trainer",
					Command = "RequestQuest"
				} }))
				local v820 = printBeltName(response)

				if debug == false and not response and not v820 then
					_tp(CFrame.new(5865.0234375, 1208.3154296875, 871.15185546875))
					debug = true
				elseif debug == true and (CFrame.new(5865.0234375, 1208.3154296875, 871.15185546875).Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 50 then
					if v820 == "White" then
						local v821 = GetConnectionEnemies("Skull Slayer")

						if v821 then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								t9.Kill(v821, _G.Dojoo)

								if response then
									if not _G.Dojoo or not t9.Alive(v821) then
										break
									end
								else
									break
								end
							end
						else
							_tp(CFrame.new(-16759.58984375, 71.283767700195312, 1595.3399658203125))
						end
					elseif v820 == "Yellow" then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							_G.SeaBeast1 = true
							_G.TerrorShark = true
							_G.Shark = true
							_G.Piranha = true
							_G.MobCrew = true
							_G.FishBoat = true
							_G.SailBoats = true

							if _G.Dojoo then
								if not response then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end

						_G.SeaBeast1 = false
						_G.TerrorShark = false
						_G.Shark = false
						_G.Piranha = false
						_G.MobCrew = false
						_G.FishBoat = false
						_G.SailBoats = false
					elseif v820 == "Green" then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							_G.SailBoats = true

							if _G.Dojoo then
								if not response then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end

						_G.SailBoats = false
					elseif v820 == "Purple" then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							_G.FarmEliteHunt = true

							if _G.Dojoo then
								if not response then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end

						_G.FarmEliteHunt = false
					elseif v820 == "Red" then
						while true do
							task.wait()
							_G.SailBoats = true
							_G.FishBoat = true

							if _G.Dojoo then
								if not response then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end

						_G.SailBoats = false
						_G.FishBoat = false
					elseif v820 == "Black" then
						repeat
							task.wait()

							local ActivationPrompt

							if workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland") then
								_G.Prehis_Find = true
								ActivationPrompt = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.ActivationPrompt

								if ActivationPrompt:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt", true) then
									_G.Prehis_Skills = false
									_G.Prehis_Find = true
								else
									_G.Prehis_Skills = true
									_G.Prehis_Find = false
								end

								if not _G.Dojoo or not response then
									break
								end
							elseif workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Prehistoric Island") then
								_G.Prehis_Find = true
								ActivationPrompt = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.ActivationPrompt

								if ActivationPrompt:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt", true) then
									_G.Prehis_Skills = false
									_G.Prehis_Find = true
								else
									_G.Prehis_Skills = true
									_G.Prehis_Find = false
								end
							else
								_G.Prehis_Find = true
								_G.Prehis_Skills = false
							end
						until not _G.Dojoo or not response

						_G.Prehis_Find = false
						_G.Prehis_Skills = false
					elseif v820 == "Orange" or v820 == "Blue" then
						return nil
					end
				end

				if not response then
					debug = false
					replicated.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest"):InvokeServer(unpack({ {
						NPC = "Dojo Trainer",
						Command = "ClaimQuest"
					} }))
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
BlazeEM = t15.Prehistoric:AddToggle({

checkQuesta = function()
	local t34 = { {
		Context = "Check"
	} }
	local response

	pcall(function()
		game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RF/DragonHunter"):InvokeServer(unpack({ {
			Context = "RequestQuest"
		} }))
	end)

	local _ = pcall(function()
		response = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RF/DragonHunter"):InvokeServer(unpack(t34))
	end)
	local v827 = false
	local v828
	local v829
	local n4

	if response and response.Text then
		v827 = true

		local Text = response.Text

		if string.find(tostring(Text), "Defeat") then
			n4 = 1

			local v832 = string.sub(tostring(Text), 8, 9)

			v828 = tonumber(v832)

			for _, v in pairs({
				"Hydra Enforcer",
				"Venomous Assailant"
			}) do
				if string.find(Text, v) then
					v829 = v

					break
				end
			end
		elseif string.find(tostring(Text), "Destroy") then
			v828 = 10
			n4 = 2
			v829 = nil
		end
	end

	return v827, v829, v828, n4
end
BackTODoJo = function()
	for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren()) do
		if child.Name == "NotificationTemplate" and string.find(child.Text, "Head back to the Dojo to complete more tasks") then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end
DragonMobClear = function(p232, p233, p234)
	if workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(p233) then
		for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
			if child.Name == p233 and t9.Alive(child) and p232 then
				t9.Kill(child, p232)
			end
		end
	else
		_tp(p234)
	end
end
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.FarmBlazeEM then
			pcall(function()
				local v839, v840, _, v842 = checkQuesta()

				if v839 ~= true or BackTODoJo() then
					_tp(CFrame.new(5813, 1208, 884))
					DragonMobClear(false, nil, nil)
				elseif v842 ~= 1 then
					if v842 == 2 then
						if workspace.Map.Waterfall.IslandModel:FindFirstChild("Meshes/bambootree", true) then
							repeat
								wait()
								spawn(function()
									_tp(workspace.Map.Waterfall.IslandModel:FindFirstChild("Meshes/bambootree", true).CFrame * CFrame.new(4, 0, 0))
								end)

								if (workspace.Map.Waterfall.IslandModel:FindFirstChild("Meshes/bambootree", true).Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 200 then
									MousePos = workspace.Map.Waterfall.IslandModel:FindFirstChild("Meshes/bambootree", true).Position
									Useskills("Melee", "Z")
									Useskills("Melee", "X")
									Useskills("Melee", "C")
									wait(0.5)
									Useskills("Sword", "Z")
									Useskills("Sword", "X")
									wait(0.5)
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									wait(0.5)
									Useskills("Gun", "Z")
									Useskills("Gun", "X")
								end
							until not _G.FarmBlazeEM or BackTODoJo()
						end
					end
				elseif v840 == "Hydra Enforcer" or v840 == "Venomous Assailant" then
					while true do
						wait()
						DragonMobClear(true, v840, CFrame.new(4620.61572265625, 1002.2954711914062, 399.08688354492188))

						if _G.FarmBlazeEM then
							if BackTODoJo() then
								break
							end
						else
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	while wait(0.1) do
		if _G.FarmBlazeEM then
			pcall(function()
				if workspace.EmberTemplate:FindFirstChild("Part") then
					game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = workspace.EmberTemplate.Part.CFrame
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.UPGDrago then
				if GetQuestDracoLevel() == false then
					return nil
				end

				if GetQuestDracoLevel() == true then
					if (CFrame.new(5814.42724609375, 1208.3267822265625, 884.57855224609375).Position - Root.Position).Magnitude >= 300 then
						_tp(CFrame.new(5814.42724609375, 1208.3267822265625, 884.57855224609375))
					else
						_tp(CFrame.new(5814.42724609375, 1208.3267822265625, 884.57855224609375))
						replicated.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest"):InvokeServer(unpack({ {
							NPC = "Dragon Wizard",
							Command = "Upgrade"
						} }))
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.DragoV1 and GetM("Dragon Egg") <= 0 then
				while true do
					wait()
					_G.Prehis_Find = true
					_G.Prehis_Skills = true
					_G.Prehis_DE = true

					if _G.DragoV1 then
						if GetM("Dragon Egg") >= 1 then

				end

				_G.Prehis_DE = false
			end
		end)
	end
end)

local u856

spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Prehis_Find then
				local Character = plr.Character

				if not Character then
					return
				end

				local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

				if not HumanoidRootPart or not Humanoid or Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					return
				end

				local PrehistoricIsland = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Prehistoric Island", true)

				if PrehistoricIsland then
					local HeadTeleport = PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("HeadTeleport", true) or PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Teleport_Head", true) or PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Head", true)

					if HeadTeleport then
						local CFrame_ = HeadTeleport.CFrame
						local v863 = CFrame_.Position - CFrame_.LookVector * 40 + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)

						if (v863 - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
							_tp(CFrame.new(v863))
						end
					else
						local Position = PrehistoricIsland.CFrame.Position

						_tp(CFrame.new(Position - (Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Unit * 250 + Vector3.new(0, 60, 0)))
					end
				else
					local v865 = CheckBoat()

					if not v865 then
						local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)

						TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

						if (cFrame3.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat or "Guardian")
						end

						return
					end

					if Humanoid.Sit == false then
						_tp(v865.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))

						return
					end

					local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)

					u856 = cFrame3

					if CheckEnemiesBoat() or CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
						_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 150, 37016.25))
					else
						_tp(cFrame3)
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

					if workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland", true) then
						local ActivationPrompt = workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild("ActivationPrompt", true)

						if ActivationPrompt and ActivationPrompt:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
							if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(ActivationPrompt.CFrame.Position) <= 150 then
								fireproximityprompt(ActivationPrompt.ProximityPrompt, math.huge)
								vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "E", false, game)
								wait(1.5)
								vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "E", false, game)
							end

							_tp(ActivationPrompt.CFrame)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
t15.Prehistoric:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Patch Prehistoric Event",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Patch Prehistoric Event", false),
	Callback = function(p247)
		_G.Prehis_Skills = p247
		_G.SaveData["Auto Patch Prehistoric Event"] = p247
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait(0.3) do
		if _G.Prehis_Skills then
			pcall(function()
				local PrehistoricIsland = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")

				if not PrehistoricIsland then
					return
				end

				for _, descendant in pairs(PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") or descendant:IsA("MeshPart") then
						if descendant.Name:lower():find("lava") then
							descendant:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end

				local Core = PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Core")

				if Core then
					local InteriorLava = Core:FindFirstChild("InteriorLava")

					if InteriorLava then
						InteriorLava:Destroy()
					end
				end

				local TrialTeleport = PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("TrialTeleport")

				for _, descendant in pairs(PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant.Name == "TouchInterest" and (not TrialTeleport or not descendant:IsDescendantOf(TrialTeleport)) then
						descendant.Parent:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		if _G.Prehis_Skills then
			pcall(function()
				local v877 = GetConnectionEnemies("Lava Golem")

				if v877 then
					if v877:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
						while true do
							wait(0.1)
							t9.Kill(v877, true)
							v877.Humanoid:ChangeState(15)

							if _G.Prehis_Skills then
								if not v877.Parent or v877.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		if _G.Prehis_Skills then
			pcall(function()
				local PrehistoricIsland = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")

				if not PrehistoricIsland then
					return
				end

				local Core = PrehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Core")

				if not Core then
					return
				end

				local VolcanoRocks = Core:FindFirstChild("VolcanoRocks")

				if not VolcanoRocks then
					return
				end

				for _, child in pairs(VolcanoRocks:GetChildren()) do
					local VFXLayer = child:FindFirstChild("VFXLayer")
					local At0 = VFXLayer and VFXLayer:FindFirstChild("At0")
					local Glow = At0 and At0:FindFirstChild("Glow")

					if Glow and Glow.Enabled then
						repeat
							wait(0.1)
							_tp(VFXLayer.CFrame)

							if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(VFXLayer.CFrame.Position) <= 150 then
								MousePos = VFXLayer.CFrame.Position
								Useskills("Melee", "Z")
								wait(0.4)
								Useskills("Melee", "X")
								wait(0.4)
								Useskills("Melee", "C")
								wait(0.4)
								Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
								wait(0.4)
								Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
								wait(0.4)
								Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
							end

							if not _G.Prehis_Skills then
								break
							end
						until not Glow.Enabled
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

			pcall(function()

				local Character = LocalPlayer4.Character
				local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not HumanoidRootPart then
					return
				end

				sethiddenproperty(LocalPlayer4, "SimulationRadius", math.huge)

				for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
					if child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and (child.HumanoidRootPart.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 500 and child.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
						child.Humanoid.Health = 0
						child.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
						child:BreakJoints()
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

	Default = false,
	Callback = function(p252)
		getgenv().SpeedBoat = p252
	end
})
game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	if getgenv().SpeedBoat then
		local LocalPlayer4 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer

		if LocalPlayer4.Character then
			if LocalPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and LocalPlayer4.Character.Humanoid.Sit then
				for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Boats:GetChildren()) do
					local VehicleSeat = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat")

					if VehicleSeat then
						VehicleSeat.MaxSpeed = SetSpeedBoat
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)
t15.SeaEvent:AddSlider({
	Name = "Ship Speed",
	Min = 0,
	Max = 1000,
	Increment = 1,
	Default = 300,
	Callback = function(p253)
		SetSpeedBoat = p253
	end
})
t15.SeaEvent:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Press W",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Press W", false),
	Callback = function(p254)
		getgenv().AutoPressW = p254
		_G.SaveData["Auto Press W"] = p254
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if getgenv().AutoPressW then
				if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid").Sit == true then
					game:GetService("VirtualInputManager"):SendKeyEvent(true, "W", false, game)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
t15.SeaEvent:AddToggle({
	Name = "No Clip Ship",
	Default = GetSetting("No Clip Ship", false),
	Callback = function(p255)
		getgenv().NoClipShip = p255
		_G.SaveData["No Clip Ship"] = p255
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		pcall(function()
			for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Boats:GetChildren()) do
				for _, descendant in pairs(child:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						if getgenv().NoClipShip or getgenv().FindPrehistoric then
							descendant.CanCollide = false
						else
							descendant.CanCollide = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

	Callback = function(p257)

Q = t15.SeaEvent:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Sail Boat",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Sail Boat", false),
	Callback = function(p258)
		_G.SailBoats = p258
		_G.SaveData["Auto Sail Boat"] = p258
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.SailBoats then
			pcall(function()
				local v905 = CheckBoat()

				if not v905 and (not CheckShark() or not _G.Shark) and (not CheckTerrorShark() or not _G.TerrorShark) and (not CheckFishCrew() or not _G.MobCrew) and (not CheckPiranha() or not _G.Piranha) and (not CheckEnemiesBoat() or not _G.FishBoat) and (not CheckSeaBeast() or not _G.SeaBeast1) and (not _G.PGB or not CheckPirateGrandBrigade()) and (not _G.HCM or not CheckHauntedCrew()) and (not _G.Leviathan1 or not CheckLeviathan()) then
					local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)

					TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

					if (cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
						replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat)
					end
				elseif v905 and (not CheckShark() or not _G.Shark) and (not CheckTerrorShark() or not _G.TerrorShark) and (not CheckFishCrew() or not _G.MobCrew) then
					if (not CheckPiranha() or not _G.Piranha) and (not CheckEnemiesBoat() or not _G.FishBoat) and (not CheckSeaBeast() or not _G.SeaBeast1) and (not _G.PGB or not CheckPirateGrandBrigade()) and (not _G.HCM or not CheckHauntedCrew()) and (not _G.Leviathan1 or not CheckLeviathan()) then
						if plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
							_tp(v905.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))
						else
							if _G.DangerSc == "Lv 1" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-21998.375, 30.0006084, -682.309143)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 2" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-26779.5215, 30.0005474, -822.858032)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 3" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-31171.957, 30.0001011, -2256.93774)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 4" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-34054.6875, 30.2187767, -2560.12012)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 5" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-38887.5547, 30.0004578, -2162.99023)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv 6" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-44541.7617, 30.0003204, -1244.8584)
							elseif _G.DangerSc == "Lv Infinite" then
								CFrameSelectedZone = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)
							end

							while true do
								wait()

								if not _G.FishBoat and CheckEnemiesBoat() or not _G.PGB and CheckPirateGrandBrigade() or not _G.TerrorShark and CheckTerrorShark() then
									_tp(CFrameSelectedZone * CFrame.new(0, 150, 0))
								else
									_tp(CFrameSelectedZone)
								end

								if _G.SailBoats ~= false and (not CheckShark() or not _G.Shark) and (not CheckTerrorShark() or not _G.TerrorShark) and (not CheckFishCrew() or not _G.MobCrew) and (not CheckPiranha() or not _G.Piranha) and (not CheckSeaBeast() or not _G.SeaBeast1) and (not CheckEnemiesBoat() or not _G.FishBoat) and (not _G.Leviathan1 or not CheckLeviathan()) and (not _G.HCM or not CheckHauntedCrew()) and (not _G.PGB or not CheckPirateGrandBrigade()) then
									if plr.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid").Sit ~= false then
										continue
									end
								end

								break
							end

							plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
spawn(function()
	while wait(1.5) do
		pcall(function()
			for _, child in pairs(workspace.Boats:GetChildren()) do
				for _, descendant in pairs(workspace.Boats[child.Name]:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						if _G.SailBoats or _G.Prehis_Find or _G.FindMirage or _G.SailBoat_Hydra or _G.AutofindKitIs then
							descendant.CanCollide = false
						else
							descendant.CanCollide = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)

t15.SeaEvent:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Attack Sea Beast",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Attack Sea Beast", false),
	Callback = function(p266)
		_G.SeaBeast1 = p266
		_G.SaveData["Auto Attack Sea Beast"] = p266
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		pcall(function()
			if _G.Shark then
				local t37 = { "Shark" }

				if CheckShark() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(t37, child.Name) and t9.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								t9.Kill(child, _G.Shark)

								if _G.Shark == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.TerrorShark then
				local t38 = { "Terrorshark" }

				if CheckTerrorShark() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(t38, child.Name) and t9.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								t9.KillSea(child, _G.TerrorShark)

								if _G.TerrorShark == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.Piranha then
				local t39 = { "Piranha" }

				if CheckPiranha() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(t39, child.Name) then
							if t9.Alive(child) then
								while true do
									task.wait()
									t9.Kill(child, _G.Piranha)

									if _G.Piranha == false then
										break
									elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.MobCrew then
				local t40 = { "Fish Crew Member" }

				if CheckFishCrew() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(t40, child.Name) and t9.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								t9.Kill(child, _G.MobCrew)

								if _G.MobCrew == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.HCM then
				local t41 = { "Haunted Crew Member" }

				if CheckHauntedCrew() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if table.find(t41, child.Name) and t9.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								t9.Kill(child, _G.HCM)

								if _G.HCM == false then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.SeaBeast1 then
				if workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild("SeaBeast1") then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								spawn(function()
									_tp(CFrame.new(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, game:GetService("Workspace").Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
								end)

								if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position) <= 500 then
									AitSeaSkill_Custom = child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
									MousePos = AitSeaSkill_Custom.Position

									if CheckF() then
										weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									else
										Useskills("Melee", "Z")
										Useskills("Melee", "X")
										Useskills("Melee", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Sword", "Z")
										Useskills("Sword", "X")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Gun", "Z")
										Useskills("Gun", "X")
									end
								end
							until _G.SeaBeast1 == false or not child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or not child.Parent or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.Leviathan1 then
				if workspace.SeaBeasts:FindFirstChild("Leviathan") then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Leviathan Segment") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								spawn(function()
									_tp(CFrame.new(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, game:GetService("Workspace").Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
								end)

								if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.Position) <= 500 then
									MousePos = child:FindFirstChild("Leviathan Segment").Position

									if CheckF() then
										weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									else
										Useskills("Melee", "Z")
										Useskills("Melee", "X")
										Useskills("Melee", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Sword", "Z")
										Useskills("Sword", "X")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Gun", "Z")
										Useskills("Gun", "X")
									end
								end
							until _G.Leviathan1 == false or not child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or not child.Parent or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.FishBoat and CheckEnemiesBoat() then
				for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
					if child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 and child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
						repeat
							task.wait()
							spawn(function()
								if child.Name == "FishBoat" then
									_tp(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -50, -25))
								end
							end)

							if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.Engine.CFrame.Position) <= 150 then
								AitSeaSkill_Custom = child.Engine.CFrame
								MousePos = AitSeaSkill_Custom.Position

								if CheckF() then
									weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
								else
									Useskills("Melee", "Z")
									Useskills("Melee", "X")
									Useskills("Melee", "C")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Sword", "Z")
									Useskills("Sword", "X")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
									Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									wait(0.1)
									Useskills("Gun", "Z")
									Useskills("Gun", "X")
								end
							end
						until _G.FishBoat == false or not child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") or child.Health.Value <= 0
					end
				end
			end

			if _G.PGB and CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
				for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
					if child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
						if child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								spawn(function()
									if child.Name == "PirateBrigade" then
										_tp(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -30, -10))
									elseif child.Name == "PirateGrandBrigade" then
										_tp(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -50, -50))
									end
								end)

								if plr:DistanceFromCharacter(child.Engine.CFrame.Position) <= 150 then
									AitSeaSkill_Custom = child.Engine.CFrame
									MousePos = AitSeaSkill_Custom.Position

									if CheckF() then
										weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
									else
										Useskills("Melee", "Z")
										Useskills("Melee", "X")
										Useskills("Melee", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Sword", "Z")
										Useskills("Sword", "X")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
										Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
										wait(0.1)
										Useskills("Gun", "Z")
										Useskills("Gun", "X")
									end
								end
							until _G.PGB == false or not child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end)
t15.SeaEvent:AddSection("Kitsune Island / Event")

local v934 = t15.SeaEvent:AddParagraph("Kitsune Island Status", "")

spawn(function()
	while wait(0.2) do
		if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("KitsuneIsland") then
			if not workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island") then
				v934:SetDesc("Kitsune Island : False")

				continue
			end
		end

		v934:SetDesc("Kitsune Island : True")
	end
end)
t15.SeaEvent:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Find Kitsune Island",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Find Kitsune Island", false),
	Callback = function(p267)
		_G.AutofindKitIs = p267
		_G.SaveData["Auto Find Kitsune Island"] = p267
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
spawn(function()
	while wait() do
		if _G.AutofindKitIs then
			pcall(function()
				if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island", true) then
					_tp(workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island").CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 500, 0))
				else
					local v935 = CheckBoat()

					if not v935 then
						local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)

						TeleportToTarget(cFrame3)

						if (cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
							replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat)
						end
					elseif plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit == false then
						_tp(v935.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))
					else
						local cFrame3 = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)

						while true do
							wait()

							if CheckEnemiesBoat() or CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
								_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 150, 37016.25))
							else
								_tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25))
							end

							if _G.AutofindKitIs and not ((cFrame3.Position - plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10) then
								if not workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island") and plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit ~= false then
									continue
								end
							end

							break
						end

						plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

spawn(function()
	while wait(0.1) do
		if _G.tweenShrine then
			pcall(function()
				local KitsuneIsland = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("KitsuneIsland") or game.Workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island")
				local ShrineActive = KitsuneIsland:FindFirstChild("ShrineActive")

				if ShrineActive then
					local next_ = next
					local descendants, v942 = ShrineActive:GetDescendants()

					for _, v in next_, descendants, v942 do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") then
							if v.Name:find("NeonShrinePart") then
								replicated.Modules.Net:FindFirstChild("RE/TouchKitsuneStatue"):FireServer()

								while true do
									wait()
									_tp(v.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 0))

									if _G.tweenShrine == false then
										break
									elseif not KitsuneIsland then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				else
					_tp(workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Kitsune Island").CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 500, 0))
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)

t15.Esp:AddSection("Esp")
isnil = function(p274)
	return p274 == nil
end

local function v950(p275)
	return math.floor(tonumber(p275) + 0.5)
end

Number = math.random(1, 1000000)

local LocalPlayer4 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local ReplicatedStorage3 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Team = LocalPlayer4.Team

EspPly = function()
	local children, v955 = game.Players:GetChildren()

	for _, v in next, children, v955 do
		pcall(function()
			if not isnil(v.Character) then
				if PlayerEsp then
					local ok = isnil(v.Character.Head)

					if not ok then
						ok = v.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("NameEsp" .. Number)
					end

					if ok then
						if v.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("NameEsp" .. Number) then
							v.Character.Head["NameEsp" .. Number].TextLabel.Text = v.Name .. " | " .. v950((LocalPlayer4.Character.Head.Position - v.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. " M\nHealth : " .. v950(v.Character.Humanoid.Health * 100 / v.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth) .. "%"
						end
					else
						local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui", v.Character.Head)

						BillboardGui.Name = "NameEsp" .. Number
						BillboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
						BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
						BillboardGui.Adornee = v.Character.Head
						BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

						local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", BillboardGui)

						TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
						TextLabel.FontSize = "Size14"
						TextLabel.TextWrapped = true
						TextLabel.Text = v.Name .. " \n" .. v950((LocalPlayer4.Character.Head.Position - v.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. " M"
						TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
						TextLabel.TextYAlignment = "Top"
						TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5

						if v.Team == Team then
							TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 254)
						else
							TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255, 0, 0)
						end
					end
				elseif v.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("NameEsp" .. Number) then
					v.Character.Head:FindFirstChild("NameEsp" .. Number):Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

ChestEsp = function()
	if ChestESP then
		local tagged = game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("_ChestTagged")

		for _, v in ipairs(tagged) do
			pcall(function()
				local Magnitude = (v:GetPivot().Position - LocalPlayer4.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude

				v:GetFullName():gsub("[^%w_]", "_")

				local ChestEspAttachment = v:FindFirstChild("ChestEspAttachment")

				if not ChestEspAttachment then
					local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")

					Attachment.Name = "ChestEspAttachment"
					Attachment.Parent = v
					Attachment.Position = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

					local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

					BillboardGui.Name = "NameEsp"
					BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
					BillboardGui.Adornee = Attachment
					BillboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
					BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
					BillboardGui.Parent = Attachment

					local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

					TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
					TextLabel.TextSize = 14
					TextLabel.TextWrapped = true
					TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
					TextLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
					TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
					TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
					TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)
					TextLabel.Parent = BillboardGui
				end

				local NameEsp = ChestEspAttachment and ChestEspAttachment:FindFirstChild("NameEsp")

				if NameEsp then
					local v1006 = math.floor(Magnitude / 3)

					NameEsp.TextLabel.Text = string.format("[%s] %d M", v.Name:gsub("Label", ""), v1006)
				end
			end)
		end
	else
		for _, v in ipairs(game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("_ChestTagged")) do
			local ChestEspAttachment = v:FindFirstChild("ChestEspAttachment")

			if ChestEspAttachment then
				ChestEspAttachment:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end
berriesEsp = function()
	if BerryEsp then
		local tagged = game:GetService("CollectionService"):GetTagged("BerryBush")

		for _, v in ipairs(tagged) do
			pcall(function()
				local Position = v.Parent:GetPivot().Position

				for _, v2 in pairs(v:GetAttributes()) do
					if v2 then
						local name = "BerryEspPart_" .. v2 .. "_" .. tostring(Position)
						local Part2 = workspace:FindFirstChild(name)

						if not Part2 then
							Part2 = Instance.new("Part")
							Part2.Name = name
							Part2.Transparency = 1
							Part2.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
							Part2.Anchored = true
							Part2.CanCollide = false
							Part2.Parent = workspace
							Part2.CFrame = CFrame.new(Position)
						end

						if not Part2:FindFirstChild("NameEsp") then
							local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui", Part2)

							BillboardGui.Name = "NameEsp"
							BillboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
							BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 30)
							BillboardGui.Adornee = Part2
							BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

							local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", BillboardGui)

							TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Code
							TextLabel.TextSize = 14
							TextLabel.TextWrapped = true
							TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
							TextLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
							TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
							TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
							TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)
						end

						local NameEsp = Part2:FindFirstChild("NameEsp")
						local v1021 = (LocalPlayer4.Character.Head.Position - Position).Magnitude / 3

						if NameEsp then
							NameEsp.TextLabel.Text = "[" .. v2 .. "]" .. " " .. math.round(v1021) .. " M"
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	else
		for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Part") then
				if child.Name:match("BerryEspPart_.*") then
					child:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
t15.Esp:AddToggle({
	Name = "Esp Berry",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Esp Berry", false),
	Callback = function(p276)
		BerryEsp = p276
		_G.SaveData["Esp Berry"] = p276
		SaveSettings()

		if p276 then
			task.spawn(function()
				while BerryEsp do
					berriesEsp()
					task.wait()
				end
			end)
		else
			for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Part") then
					if child.Name:match("BerryEspPart_.*") then
						child:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
})

t15.Raids:AddSection("Fruits Options")

local function v1050(p287)
	local v1051 = tostring(p287)
	local v1052

	repeat
		v1051, v1052 = v1051:gsub("^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")
	until v1052 == 0

	return v1051
end
local function v1053()
	local s12 = "Advance Fruit Stock\n"
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits", true)
	end)

	if ok and result then
		local v1057 = false

		for _, v in pairs(result) do
			if v.OnSale then
				v1057 = true
				s12 ..= v.Name .. " - $" .. v1050(v.Price) .. "\n"
			end
		end

		if not v1057 then
			s12 ..= "- No fruit.\n"
		end
	else
		s12 ..= "- Error while retrieving data.\n"
	end

	local v1060 = s12 .. "\nNormal Fruit Stock\n"
	local ok2, result2 = pcall(function()
		return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")
	end)

	if ok2 and result2 then
		local v1063 = false

		for _, v in pairs(result2) do
			if v.OnSale then
				v1063 = true
				v1060 ..= v.Name .. " - $" .. v1050(v.Price) .. "\n"
			end
		end

		if not v1063 then
			v1060 ..= "- No fruit.\n"
		end
	else
		v1060 ..= "- Error while retrieving data.\n"
	end

	return v1060
end

local v1066 = t15.Raids:AddParagraph("Stock Fruit", "Loading...")

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(60) do
		pcall(function()
			v1066:SetDesc(v1053())
		end)
	end
end)
pcall(function()
	v1066:SetDesc(v1053())
end)

t15.Raids:AddButton({
	Name = "Buy Dungeon Chips [Devil Fruit]",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		if GetBP("Special Microchip") then
			return
		end

		local t43 = {}
		local response, v1074 = ReplicatedStorage3:WaitForChild("Remotes").CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")

		for _, v in next, response, v1074 do
			if v.Price <= 490000 then
				table.insert(t43, v.Name)
			end
		end

		for _, v in pairs(t43) do
			for _ in pairs(t42) do
				if not GetBP("Special Microchip") then
					ReplicatedStorage3.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadFruit", tostring(v))
					ReplicatedStorage3.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaidsNpc", "Select", _G.SelectChip)
				end
			end
		end
	end
})

AutoChipFruit = t15.Raids:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Buy Chip [Devil Fruit]",
	Description = "",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Buy Chip [Devil Fruit]", false),
	Callback = function(p299)
		_G.AutoChipFruit = p299
		_G.SaveData["Auto Buy Chip [Devil Fruit]"] = p299
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1) do
		if _G.AutoChipFruit then
			pcall(function()
				if not GetBP("Special Microchip") then
					local response = ReplicatedStorage3.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")
					local Name

					for _, v in pairs(response) do
						if v.Price <= 490000 then
							Name = v.Name

							break
						end
					end

					if Name then
						ReplicatedStorage3.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadFruit", tostring(Name))
						ReplicatedStorage3.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("RaidsNpc", "Select", _G.SelectChip)
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
StartR = t15.Raids:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Start Raid",
	Description = "",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(p300)
		_G.Auto_StartRaid = p300
	end
})
task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(1.5) do
		if _G.Auto_StartRaid then
			pcall(function()
				local PlayerGui = game.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
				local Main = PlayerGui and PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Main")
				local TopHUDList = Main and Main:FindFirstChild("TopHUDList")

				if TopHUDList and TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
					return
				end

				if not GetBP("Special Microchip") then
					return
				end

				if World2 then
					local Main2 = workspace.Map.CircleIsland.RaidSummon2.Button.Main

					if Main2 then
						if Main2:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
							fireproximityprompt(Main2.ProximityPrompt)
						elseif Main2:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector") then
							fireclickdetector(Main2.ClickDetector)
						end
					end
				end

				if World3 then
					local Main2 = workspace.Map["Boat Castle"].RaidSummon2.Button.Main

					if Main2 then
						if Main2:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt") then
							fireproximityprompt(Main2.ProximityPrompt)
						elseif Main2:FindFirstChild("ClickDetector") then
							fireclickdetector(Main2.ClickDetector)
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end
end)
Raiding = t15.Raids:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Raid + Next Island",
	Description = "Tự động start raid, di chuyển đảo và Aura Kill (chỉ Island 4 & 5)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(p301)
		_G.Raiding = p301

		if not p301 then
			_G.AuraKill = false
			shouldTween = false

			local Character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character

			if Character then
				if Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("BodyClip") then
					Character.HumanoidRootPart.BodyClip:Destroy()
				end

				if Character:FindFirstChild("highlight") then
					Character.highlight:Destroy()
				end

				for _, descendant in pairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						descendant.CanCollide = true
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
})

if _G.AuraRadius == nil then
	_G.AuraRadius = 5000
end

if _G.AuraKill == nil then
	_G.AuraKill = false
end

local t44 = {
	"Island 4",
	"Island 5"
}
local Locations = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations
local t45 = {
	"Island 1",
	"Island 2",
	"Island 3",
	"Island 4",
	"Island 5"
}

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.3) do
		if _G.Raiding then
			pcall(function()
				local Character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character

				if not Character then
					return
				end

				local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not HumanoidRootPart then
					return
				end

				local v1097
				local n5 = 999999

				for _, v in ipairs(t45) do
					if not _G.Raiding then
						break
					end

					local v1101 = Locations:FindFirstChild(v)

					if v1101 then
						local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - v1101.Position).Magnitude

						if Magnitude < n5 then
							n5 = Magnitude
							v1097 = v
						end
					end
				end

				if not _G.Raiding then
					return
				end

				local auraKill = false

				for _, v in ipairs(t44) do
					if not _G.Raiding then
						break
					end

					if v1097 == v then
						auraKill = true

						break
					end
				end

				_G.AuraKill = auraKill

				if _G.AuraKill then
					local Position = HumanoidRootPart.Position
					local AuraRadius = _G.AuraRadius
					local v1108 = AuraRadius * AuraRadius

					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if not _G.Raiding then
							break
						end

						local HumanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if HumanoidRootPart2 then
							local Humanoid = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

							if Humanoid and not (Humanoid.Health <= 0) then
								local v1113 = HumanoidRootPart2.Position.X - Position.X
								local v1114 = HumanoidRootPart2.Position.Z - Position.Z
								local v1115 = HumanoidRootPart2.Position.Y - Position.Y

								if v1113 * v1113 + v1114 * v1114 + v1115 * v1115 <= v1108 then
									Humanoid.Health = 0
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			_G.AuraKill = false
		end
	end
end)
task.spawn(function()
	local v1116

	while task.wait(0.3) do
		if _G.Raiding and game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer.Visible then
			local Character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character

			if Character then
				local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if HumanoidRootPart and Humanoid and not (Humanoid.Health <= 0) and not Humanoid.Sit and not Humanoid.PlatformStand and not HumanoidRootPart.Anchored then
					local n6 = 999999

					for _, v in ipairs(t45) do
						if not _G.Raiding then
							break
						end

						local v1123 = Locations:FindFirstChild(v)

						if v1123 then
							local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart.Position - v1123.Position).Magnitude

							if Magnitude < n6 then
								n6 = Magnitude
								v1116 = v
							end
						end
					end

					if _G.Raiding and v1116 then
						local v1125 = Locations:FindFirstChild(v1116)

						if v1125 then
							local v1126 = false

							for _, child in ipairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
								if not _G.Raiding then
									break
								end

								local Humanoid2 = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
								local HumanoidRootPart2 = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

								if Humanoid2 and HumanoidRootPart2 and Humanoid2.Health > 0 and (HumanoidRootPart2.Position - v1125.Position).Magnitude < 450 then
									v1126 = true

									repeat
										task.wait()

										if not _G.Raiding then
											break
										end

										shouldTween = true
										t9.Kill(child, _G.Raiding)
									until not _G.Raiding or not child.Parent or Humanoid2.Health <= 0
								end
							end

							if _G.Raiding and not v1126 then
								local v1131 = table.find(t45, v1116)

								if v1131 and t45[v1131 + 1] then
									local v1132 = Locations:FindFirstChild(t45[v1131 + 1])

									if v1132 then
										if not _G.Raiding then
											continue
										end

										local v1133 = v1132.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 45, 120)

										shouldTween = true
										_tp(v1133)
									end

									v1116 = t45[v1131 + 1]
									task.wait(1)
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

						break

						while true do
							task.wait()
							t9.Kill(child, true)

							if _G.AutoFarmDungeon then
								if not child.Parent or Humanoid2.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							else
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end

local function v1167()
	local v1168 = v1136()

	if not v1168 then
		return
	end

	local Root_
	local huge = math.huge

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Map.Dungeon:GetChildren()) do
		local ExitTeleporter = child:FindFirstChild("ExitTeleporter")

		if ExitTeleporter and ExitTeleporter:FindFirstChild("Root") then
			local Magnitude = (v1168.Position - ExitTeleporter.Root.Position).Magnitude

			if Magnitude < huge then
				huge = Magnitude
				Root_ = ExitTeleporter.Root
			end
		end
	end

	return Root_
end

			if game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false then
				task.wait(0.5)
				game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("PlayerHunter")
			else
				for _, child in pairs(game:GetService("Workspace").Characters:GetChildren()) do
					if string.find(game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text, child.Name) then
						repeat
							task.wait()

							if AutoHaki then
								AutoHaki()
							end

							if EquipWeapon then
								EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
							end

							Useskill = true
							_tp(child.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(1, 7, 3))
							child.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
							game:GetService("VirtualUser"):CaptureController()
							game:GetService("VirtualUser"):Button1Down(Vector2.new(1280, 672))
						until _G.AutoPlayerHunter == false or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0

						Useskill = false
						game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

local Players3 = game:GetService("Players")

game:GetService("UserInputService")

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer6 = Players3.LocalPlayer
local u1204 = false
local n7 = 500
local connection
local t47 = {
	f = 0,
	b = 0,
	l = 0,
	r = 0
}
local BodyGyro
local BodyVelocity

local function v1210()
	local function v1211()
		local Character = LocalPlayer6.Character

		if not Character then
			return
		end

		local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if not Humanoid then
			return
		end

		local MoveDirection = Humanoid.MoveDirection

		t47.f = 0
		t47.b = 0
		t47.l = 0
		t47.r = 0

		if MoveDirection.Z < -0.1 then
			t47.f = 1
		elseif MoveDirection.Z > 0.1 then
			t47.b = 1
		end

		if MoveDirection.X < -0.1 then
			t47.l = 1
		elseif MoveDirection.X > 0.1 then
			t47.r = 1
		end
	end

	local connection2

	connection2 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if u1204 then
			v1211()
		elseif connection2 then
			connection2:Disconnect()
		end
	end)
end
local function v1216(p323)
	u1204 = p323

	if u1204 then
		if not LocalPlayer6.Character then
			return
		end

		local Humanoid = LocalPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local Torso

		if LocalPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
			Torso = LocalPlayer6.Character.Torso
		else
			Torso = LocalPlayer6.Character.UpperTorso
		end

		if not Humanoid or not Torso then
			return
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(LocalPlayer6.Character:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
				descendant.CanCollide = false
				descendant.Massless = true
			end
		end

		LocalPlayer6.Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
			if u1204 then
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
					descendant.Massless = true
				end
			end
		end)
		BodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro", Torso)
		BodyGyro.P = 90000
		BodyGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		BodyGyro.cframe = Torso.CFrame
		BodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", Torso)
		BodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		BodyVelocity.maxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
		v1210()
		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not u1204 or not LocalPlayer6.Character then
				return
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(LocalPlayer6.Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.CanCollide then
					descendant.CanCollide = false
				end
			end

			if t47.l + t47.r == 0 then
				if t47.f + t47.b ~= 0 then
					BodyVelocity.velocity = (workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (t47.f + t47.b) + (workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(t47.l + t47.r, (t47.f + t47.b) * 0.2, 0).p - workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p)) * n7
					BodyGyro.cframe = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame

					return
				end

				BodyVelocity.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
			else
				BodyVelocity.velocity = (workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.lookVector * (t47.f + t47.b) + (workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame * CFrame.new(t47.l + t47.r, (t47.f + t47.b) * 0.2, 0).p - workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame.p)) * n7
			end

			BodyGyro.cframe = workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame
		end)
	else
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		if LocalPlayer6.Character then
			local Humanoid = LocalPlayer6.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if Humanoid then
				Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(LocalPlayer6.Character:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
					descendant.CanCollide = true
					descendant.Massless = false
				end
			end

			if BodyGyro then
				BodyGyro:Destroy()
			end

			if BodyVelocity then
				BodyVelocity:Destroy()
			end
		end

		t47 = {
			f = 0,
			b = 0,
			l = 0,
			r = 0
		}
	end
end
local function v1226(p324)
	n7 = p324
end

	if u1204 then

		wait(0.1)
		v1216(true)
	end
end)

local function v1227()
	local Dodge = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Dodge")
	local v1229, v1230 = getgc()

	for _, v in next, v1229, v1230 do
		if typeof(v) == "function" then
			if getfenv(v).script == Dodge then
				local next_ = next
				local v1234, v1235 = getupvalues(v)

				for k, v2 in next_, v1234, v1235 do
					if tostring(v2) == "0.4" then
						setupvalue(v, k, 0)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local s13 = "Dark Step"
local u1251 = false

local function v1252()
	local t49 = {}

	for k, v in pairs(t48) do
		if v.Pos then
			table.insert(t49, k)
		end
	end

	table.sort(t49)

	return t49
end

t15.Shop:AddSection("Style Auto Buy")
t15.Shop:AddDropdown({
	Name = "Choose Melee To Auto Buy",
	Options = v1252(),
	Default = "Dark Step",
	Callback = function(p339)
		s13 = p339
		u1251 = false
	end
})
t15.Shop:AddToggle({
	Name = "Auto Buy Melee",
	Description = "Tự động di chuyển và mua Melee đã chọn",
	Default = GetSetting("Auto Buy Melee", false),
	Callback = function(p340)
		_G.AutoBuyMelee = p340
		u1251 = false
		_G.SaveData["Auto Buy Melee"] = p340
		SaveSettings()
	end
})
Spawn(function()
	while task.wait(0.5) do
		if _G.AutoBuyMelee then
			pcall(function()
				local v1256 = t48[s13]

				if not v1256 or not v1256.Pos then
					_G.AutoBuyMelee = false
					u1251 = false

					return
				end

				local Character = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
				local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not HumanoidRootPart then
					return
				end

				if (HumanoidRootPart.Position - v1256.Pos.Position).Magnitude <= 15 then
					u1251 = false

					local CommF_ = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_

					CommF_:InvokeServer(v1256.Key)

					if v1256.Key == "BuyDragonClaw" then
						CommF_:InvokeServer("BlackbeardReward", "DragonClaw", "1")
						CommF_:InvokeServer("BlackbeardReward", "DragonClaw", "2")
					elseif v1256.Key == "BuySharkmanKarate" then
						CommF_:InvokeServer("BuySharkmanKarate", true)
						CommF_:InvokeServer("BuySharkmanKarate")
					elseif v1256.Key == "BuySanguineArt" then
						CommF_:InvokeServer("SanguineArt", true)
					end

					task.wait(0.5)
				elseif not u1251 then
					u1251 = true
					shouldTween = true
					_tp(v1256.Pos)
					task.wait(0.3)
				end
			end)
		else
			u1251 = false
		end
	end
end)

t15.Misc:AddButton({
	Name = "Hop Server",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		task.spawn(function()
			local HttpService2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
			local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
			local Players4 = game:GetService("Players")
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				local response = game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")

				return HttpService2:JSONDecode(response).data
			end)

			if ok and result then
				local id

				for _, v in pairs(result) do
					if v.playing < v.maxPlayers then
						id = v.id
					end
				end

				if id then
					pcall(function()
						TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, id, Players4.LocalPlayer)
					end)
				end
			end
		end)
	end
})
t15.Misc:AddButton({
	Name = "Hop to Lowest Players",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		local HttpService2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
		local v1278 = "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. game.PlaceId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"

		ListServers = function(p341)
			local response = game:HttpGet(v1278 .. (p341 and "&cursor=" .. p341 or ""))

			return HttpService2:JSONDecode(response)
		end

		local nextPageCursor
		local v1281

		repeat
			local v1282 = ListServers(nextPageCursor)

			v1281 = v1282.data[1]
			nextPageCursor = v1282.nextPageCursor
		until v1281

		TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, v1281.id, LocalPlayer5)
	end
})
t15.Misc:AddButton({
	Name = "Hop to Lowest Pings Server",
	Description = "",
	Callback = function()
		local HttpService2 = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local TeleportService_ = game:GetService("TeleportService")
		local Stats_ = game:GetService("Stats")

		local function v1286(p342, p343)
			local v1287 = string.format("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?limit=%d", p342, p343)
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return HttpService2:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet(v1287))
			end)

			if ok and result and result.data then
				return result.data
			end

			return nil
		end

		local v1290 = v1286(game.PlaceId, 100)

		if not v1290 then
			return
		end

		local v1291 = v1290[1]

		for _, v in pairs(v1290) do
			if v.ping < v1291.ping and v.playing < v.maxPlayers then
				v1291 = v
			end
		end

		task.wait(0.5)

		if tonumber(Stats_.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValueString():match("(%d+)")) >= 100 then
			TeleportService_:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, v1291.id)
		end
	end
})

rtxM = t15.Misc:AddToggle({
	Name = "Turn on RTX Mode",
	Description = "",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(p349)
		_G.RTXMode = p349

		local ColorCorrectionEffect = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", game.Lighting)
		local ColorCorrectionEffect2 = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect", game.Lighting)

		OldAmbient = game.Lighting.Ambient
		OldBrightness = game.Lighting.Brightness
		OldColorShift_Top = game.Lighting.ColorShift_Top
		OldBrightnessc = ColorCorrectionEffect.Brightness
		OldContrastc = ColorCorrectionEffect.Contrast
		OldTintColorc = ColorCorrectionEffect.TintColor
		OldTintColore = ColorCorrectionEffect2.TintColor

		if not _G.RTXMode then
			return
		end

		while _G.RTXMode do
			wait()
			game.Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(33, 33, 33)
			game.Lighting.Brightness = 0.3
			ColorCorrectionEffect.Brightness = 0.176
			ColorCorrectionEffect.Contrast = 0.39
			ColorCorrectionEffect.TintColor = Color3.fromRGB(217, 145, 57)
			game.Lighting.FogEnd = 999

			if not LocalPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("PointLight") then
				local PointLight = Instance.new("PointLight")

				PointLight.Parent = LocalPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart
				PointLight.Range = 15
				PointLight.Color = Color3.fromRGB(217, 145, 57)
			end

			if not _G.RTXMode then
				game.Lighting.Ambient = OldAmbient
				game.Lighting.Brightness = OldBrightness
				game.Lighting.ColorShift_Top = OldColorShift_Top
				ColorCorrectionEffect.Contrast = OldContrastc
				ColorCorrectionEffect.Brightness = OldBrightnessc
				ColorCorrectionEffect.TintColor = OldTintColorc
				ColorCorrectionEffect2.TintColor = OldTintColore
				game.Lighting.FogEnd = 2500
				LocalPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("PointLight"):Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
})

local LocalPlayer7 = game.Players.LocalPlayer

local function v1310(p355)
	if not p355 then
		return false
	end

	local Humanoid = p355:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

	return Humanoid and Humanoid.Health > 0
end
local function v1312(p356, p357)
	local children = game:GetService("Workspace").Enemies:GetChildren()
	local players = game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()
	local t52 = {}
	local Position = p356:GetPivot().Position

	for _, child in ipairs(children) do
		local HumanoidRootPart = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if HumanoidRootPart and v1310(child) and (HumanoidRootPart.Position - Position).Magnitude <= p357 then
			table.insert(t52, child)
		end
	end

	for _, player in ipairs(players) do
		if player ~= LocalPlayer7 and player.Character then
			local HumanoidRootPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if HumanoidRootPart and v1310(player.Character) and (HumanoidRootPart.Position - Position).Magnitude <= p357 then
				table.insert(t52, player.Character)
			end
		end
	end

	return t52
end

AttackNoCoolDown = function()
	local LocalPlayer8 = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
	local Character = LocalPlayer8.Character

	if not Character then
		return
	end

	local v1325

	for _, child in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Tool") then
			v1325 = child

			break
		end
	end

	if not v1325 then
		return
	end

	local v1328 = v1312(Character, 60)

	if #v1328 == 0 then
		return
	end

	local ReplicatedStorage5 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local Modules = ReplicatedStorage5:FindFirstChild("Modules")

	if not Modules then
		return
	end

	local RERegisterAttack = ReplicatedStorage5:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RE/RegisterAttack")
	local RERegisterHit = ReplicatedStorage5:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RE/RegisterHit")

	if not RERegisterAttack or not RERegisterHit then
		return
	end

	local t53 = {}
	local v1334

	for _, v in ipairs(v1328) do
		if not v:GetAttribute("IsBoat") then
			local t54 = {
				"RightLowerArm",
				"RightUpperArm",
				"LeftLowerArm",
				"LeftUpperArm",
				"RightHand",
				"LeftHand"
			}
			local PrimaryPart = v:FindFirstChild(t54[math.random(#t54)]) or v.PrimaryPart

			if PrimaryPart then
				table.insert(t53, {
					v,
					PrimaryPart
				})
				v1334 = PrimaryPart
			end
		end
	end

	if not v1334 then
		return
	end

	RERegisterAttack:FireServer(0)

	local PlayerScripts = LocalPlayer8:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")

	if not PlayerScripts then
		return
	end

	local LocalScript = PlayerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass("LocalScript")

	while not LocalScript do
		PlayerScripts.ChildAdded:Wait()
		LocalScript = PlayerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass("LocalScript")
	end

	local SendHitsToServer

	if getsenv then
		local ok, result = pcall(getsenv, LocalScript)

		if ok and result then
			SendHitsToServer = result._G.SendHitsToServer
		end
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return require(Modules.Flags).COMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD or false
	end)

	if ok and result and SendHitsToServer then
		SendHitsToServer(v1334, t53)
	elseif ok and not result then
		RERegisterHit:FireServer(v1334, t53)
	end
end

get_Monster = function()
	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
		local UpperTorso = child:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or child:FindFirstChild("Head")

		if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) and UpperTorso and (child.Head.Position - LocalPlayer5.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 50 then
			return true, UpperTorso.Position
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
		if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
			return true, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position
		end
	end

	for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
		if child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 and child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
			return true, child.Engine.Position
		end
	end
end
Actived = function()
	local Tool = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
	local v1354, v1355 = getconnections(Tool.Activated)

	for _, v in next, v1354, v1355 do
		if typeof(v.Function) == "function" then
			getupvalues(v.Function)
		end
	end
end

				if LeftClickRemote then
					Actived()

					LeftClickRemote:FireServer(false)
				end
			end
		end)
	end)
end)

end

t55.ExecuteFastAttack = function()
	local Character = LocalPlayer8.Character

	if not Character then
		return
	end

	if not Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
		return
	end

	local v1396 = t55.GetAllTargets(Character)

	if #v1396 < 1 then
		return
	end

	local v1397 = t55.GetTargetParts(v1396)

	if #v1397 < 1 then
		return
	end

	local reRegisterHit = net["RE/RegisterHit"]

	net["RE/RegisterAttack"]:FireServer(t55.Rate)
	reRegisterHit:FireServer(v1397[1][2], v1397)
end

				u1399 = child
				attribute = child:GetAttribute("Id")
			end
		end

		v.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("RemoteEvent") and child:GetAttribute("Id") then
				u1399 = child
				attribute = child:GetAttribute("Id")
			end
		end)
	end
end
